# CODE inbox · re-stamped 9 August 2026, thirty-second stamp · THE HALVING IS MET AND RATIFIED · the LANE RULING (tokens versus latency is dispatcher config) · the STABILITY PROBE joins the accuracy phase · the buyer-role collision goes to Matthew

**This inbox supersedes the 4 August inbox whole. Read this top stamp and the twenty-seventh together, then the CURRENT Board (`docs/aao-board.md`, whatever its stamp says), then the current Charters (§P8 and Part II), then the Architecture heads newest-first. Version numbers never appear in this line again; the documents' own stamps are the authority, per the cross-reference law. Numbers from your BUILD_JOURNAL.**

## The new working mode, Matthew's ruling, 6 August

Matthew stops hand-carrying. This inbox is the standing channel: read it at every pickup; design's rulings land here and as addenda in the project. Design-side verification agents now audit every iteration mechanically; Matthew grades judgment only. Two standing obligations on every run report from now on:

1. **Ship the row export with every run.** Pairs (ref, family, contract, verbatim, offset, length, call 3 verdict), claims, answers, and the projection outcome lines, as CSV or JSON, delivered into the project beside the report. Design's agents verify against the frozen artifact and the regression seed; a report without its rows cannot be verified and does not count as a report.
2. **Ship stage timings per Addendum 20**: per-callout wall for call 0, each read, call 2, call 3's batches, join, projection; worst single callout named against the 120-second ceiling.

## Ninety-fifth stamp · MATTHEW RULES ALISON IS MENTOR and the ceiling arithmetic stands · THE REAL DEFECT IS NAMED: WHEN THE ONTOLOGY AND THE COUNTER DISAGREE, NOTHING SAYS WHICH WINS · the necessity of the arithmetic itself is OPEN, with the evidence that settles it named · s4 proceeds behind one static DML count

**Design session, 15 August, spliced above the ninety-fourth, never retyped. Two rulings from Matthew, one finding that neither of us had a name for until the first real run produced it, one question left open on purpose, and the sequence.**

**1 · RULED (Matthew, 15 August) · A NET OF +3 WITH NOTHING PULLING IT DOWN IS MENTOR.** This confirms the ceiling arithmetic as already built and changes no code: mentor-grade words move the counter ONE step and raise its ceiling to +3, and +3 is reached by accumulating. Rohan Deshpande at +1 after s3 is correct and reaches Mentor only if s4 and s5 keep giving him mentor-grade evidence. **The step-in-one-move alternative is declined.**

**2 · RULED (Matthew, 15 August) · ALISON REYES IS A MENTOR.** Her upheld s3 establishment is mentor-grade on its words — she routes the seller around four to six weeks of her own bank's legal process, "I would push hard for the former" — and call 3 upheld it blind with its reason. **The system read her correctly. The corpus authorship is what was wrong**: she was authored as the pure-process control, the lightest engagement, the floor that must not move, and then handed a line that meets the MENTOR definition exactly. **ARC-4's control pair is contaminated and Alison is no longer a floor control.** Matthew's own reading, recorded: she may also be Inner Circle by political control, and that is left undetermined rather than guessed. **Three of her other pairs were REFUSED with precise reasons, so the verifier was discriminating, not rubber-stamping — this is not an over-read.**

**3 · THE FINDING, and it is the first real one of the read · WHEN THE ONTOLOGY NAMES A STATE AND THE COUNTER NAMES A DIFFERENT ONE, NOTHING IN THE LEDGER SAYS WHICH WINS.** Measured on the first live run: `AAO_Claim__c` grades Alison MENTOR, verified blind and cited; `AAO_Answer__c.AAO_Support_Counter__c` reads 1; `AAO_Projected_Value__c` displays **Supporter**. Same shape on Rohan. The band boundaries were defined by the counter alone, and the ontology's verdict was never given a say in what is displayed. **So this is not the counter malfunctioning — it is a rule that was never written.** The seller is shown Supporter while the evidence row beneath says MENTOR, which is the one thing this system exists to prevent: a displayed value its own evidence does not support.

**4 · OPEN, AND MATTHEW'S TO RULE · IS THE ARITHMETIC NEEDED AT ALL?** His question, recorded because it is a real architectural argument and not a complaint: if the ontology determines the state from the words, and a blind verifier upholds or refuses it with reasons, what work is the counter doing that the ontology has not already done? **The case for keeping it:** it nets mixed evidence, it survives contention, it replays exactly in occurred order, and it makes a single quote insufficient to promote someone — the recorded specimen is the first fixture, where design read mentor-grade and Matthew graded supporter, and the ceiling arithmetic reproduced his answer. **The case against, which the run strengthens:** that resistance was built when EXTRACTION was the weak link. Call 3 now refuses 21 of 52 with cited reasons, so the discrimination moved upstream, and the counter may now be a second, blunter judge overruling a better one. **What settles it is s4 and s5, not argument:** if accumulation across three calls produces bands the ontology would not have named on its own, the arithmetic is earning its place; if it only ever lags the ontology by a call or two, it is a delay dressed as rigour. **No change is made to the counter before the stack completes.**

**5 · WHAT THE RUN PROVED, kept separate from what it found.** Sentiment landed on real speech for the first time: Tom −1 Non-Supporter, Kate +1, Rohan +1, Alison +1. The two-read merge fired on Rohan's coaching line — found by both reads, merged rather than double-counted, 24 of 78 overall, against the eighty-first stamp's 0 of 67 with the resolver uncalled. `AAO_Shadow_Person__c` wrote `SP-00000000`, its first row ever, on the third branch, carrying the anchor fields that did not exist at nine this morning, identity columns empty and the provenance boundary intact. Three located pairs about one person produced one flag; the bound key held on real data.

**6 · THE SEQUENCE.** One static count before s4, and it is a read of the code rather than a run: **how many DML statements on the pass path are conditional on prior state existing** — the update and patch legs that fire on s4 because there is something to update where s3 only inserted. The join reached 133 of 150 with no DML inside any loop, so the count is call sites firing, not rows, and branches taken is the growth vector. If the ceiling clears 150, s4 runs. If it does not, consolidation lands first, and **the lawful shape already exists**: the ninety-second stamp's ancestor, the 90-second split trigger, says a stage crossing its wall completes its remaining work in a continuation and never redoes. **DML has no equivalent rule and probably needs one.** Design meanwhile finishes the row pass over all 78 pairs and 31 claims and issues read sheet v0_4 carrying the model-leg dependency in the S4-08 / S4-09 pairing.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; LAW #1; the liveness rule; existence versus visibility; three readings of one instrument class; the tree-and-org hazard; no tuning against the expectation tables, ever; claim immutability; the corrections law; every ruling of the eightieth through ninety-fourth stamps; human edits beat the machine forever.


## Ninety-fourth stamp · THE FOUR DELIVERABLES ARE RATIFIED · 317 FLS-BLIND against 317 FLS-AWARE, every per-entity count identical — the anchor episode closes with a BILATERAL verification · ZERO declarative validation rules, 44 Apex guards · `AAO_Pair__c` IS TWO SHAPES IN ONE TABLE and every refusal branch is UNEXERCISED · Claim Basis is confirmed against the documents · THE READ RUNS NEXT

**Design session, 15 August, spliced above the ninety-third, never retyped. This stamp ratifies CODE's schema reference, records what it found, confirms Matthew's Claim Basis reading against the document set, and states the sequence. It carries one new rule and no new rulings.**

**1 · THE DIFF IS CLEAN AND THE FLS EPISODE CLOSES BILATERALLY.** 317 custom fields through the Tooling API FLS-blind; 317 through the standard API FLS-aware; all 17 per-entity counts identical. 40 picklists, 163 values, **zero inactive** — no ghosts. Nothing to grant. Design reading 21 fields on `AAO_Shadow_Person__c` and Tooling reading 21 is this morning's grant verified from the other side, by an instrument that fails differently. **The ninetieth stamp's law is satisfied in both directions for the first time.**

**2 · NEW RULE, from CODE's caveat and it is the right one · THE CHECK IS REPEATABLE, NEVER PERMANENT.** A Metadata-API-deployed field grants FLS to nobody, System Administrator included, so **the next new field is invisible to design the moment it lands.** Therefore: **every deploy that adds a field ships its `fieldPermissions` in the same deploy**, and the field count is re-run against both instruments in the report that announces it. A schema addition without its grant is not a completed deploy.

**3 · ZERO DECLARATIVE VALIDATION RULES. All 44 guards are Apex `addError` across 8 handlers, 17 on `AAO_Pair__c` alone.** This is why the org's own laws are unreadable from the metadata tree and only surface when something breaks one — which is how CODE learned two of them today, and how the schema taught us a real defect in `disposition()`. Now inventoried in `b-apex-inventory.md`. **Named debt, not glossed: two call-2 implementations still coexist, `AAO_IdentifyCharter` and `AAO_Resolve`. The twenty-eighth stamp's one-implementation rule is unsatisfied.**

**4 · `AAO_Pair__c` IS TWO PHYSICAL SHAPES IN ONE TABLE**, which is why one paragraph could never describe it: Located is forbidden the person, Identified is forbidden the located-only fields. Measured — **236 Located, 135 Identified, 134 of 135 dispositions on the happy path, and the single `Ambiguous` row is CODE's own wiring proof.** So **every refusal branch is unexercised on real data**, and the Wells Fargo read is their first exercise. Grade a refusal-branch divergence as a first walk, never as a regression.

**5 · `AAO_Shadow_Person__c` HAS NEVER HELD A ROW — zero ever, not zero lately.** The Wells Fargo read writes this object's first row during a graded run. **There is no known-good state to regress from**, so S5-18, S5-19 and DELTA-1 grade divergences there as findings about a never-walked path. This sits beside the eighty-eighth stamp's zero-coverage disclosure and does not weaken it.

**6 · CLAIM BASIS · MATTHEW'S READING IS CONFIRMED BY THE DOCUMENTS, and CODE's finding is that the link moved rather than went missing.** All 100 claims are `Basis = Transcript` and carry their spans and Source on themselves; the receipt walks end to end. The junction serves `State`, which this corpus has never produced. Model & Flow's Object Model and Field Tables §6, the Glossary and Architecture's cited-type rule all say the same thing: **a claim resting on state must name the row and quote its value**, half frozen on the junction and half live through the lookup, because a citation that only points is a citation that rots. **Matthew's Process/Assessment case is exactly it** — an assessment answer resting on two map rows plus a key-player placement plus an insight card is four basis rows, each with its own frozen snapshot — **and the claim's own `AAO_Basis__c` value for it is `Both`, defined in the org and never yet used**, because that charter reads the maps AND the source text. **The fence stays up:** `AAO_Covers_Element__c` is Text(40) and required in spirit — *if a row cannot name which part of the proposition it covers, it does not belong on the claim* — because the recorded failure mode is a claim listing everything on the deal reading as far better supported than it was. **A DOCUMENTATION DEFECT TO FIX IN THE FOLD:** Field Tables §6 declares six cited types, Object Model declares eight and names `Source` and `Line_Item` as built though neither appears in the table. CODE's dump has the truth.

**7 · THE SEQUENCE, and this is the whole of it.** The reference now EXISTS — design's inventory plus CODE's four, all in the repo. **The fold into Model & Flow and Architecture is editorial consolidation of material already captured, so it follows the read rather than blocking it.** That is a change from the ninety-third's item 5 and it is deliberate: the ninety-third's reason was that divergences cannot be classified without a current field reference, and there now is one. **Run Wells Fargo session 3 alone.** Row export and stage timings with it, per the standing obligations. Design grades s3 against `aao-wf-s3` expectations and the liveness rule, then session 4 stacked — checking that the support counter incremented off prior state, that People and Problems carried forward, that identity resolution held — then session 5. **No rehearsal, no tuning against the expectation tables, ever.**

**8 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; LAW #1; existence versus visibility; three readings of one instrument class; the tree-and-org hazard; the liveness rule and the S4-08/S4-09 pairing; the partial retirement as named debt; the counting laws; claim immutability; the corrections law; every ruling of the eightieth through ninety-third stamps; human edits beat the machine forever.


## Ninety-third stamp · MATTHEW IS RIGHT AND THE GAP IS LARGER THAN SUSPECTED · THERE IS NO CURRENT SCHEMA-AND-FLOW REFERENCE · seven of thirteen objects have NO FIELD TABLE, the documented data flow describes a RETIRED PIPELINE, there is no Apex inventory and no ALTF field map · THE WORK IS SPLIT AND CODE OWNS FOUR DELIVERABLES

**Design session, 15 August, spliced above the ninety-second, never retyped. Matthew asked whether the document set still carries the schema comprehensively. It does not. This stamp records the audit, names CODE's half, and states why this comes before the Wells Fargo runs.**

**1 · THE AUDIT, run against Architecture v4.12, Model & Flow v1.5 and the Board, all three read in full.** The findings, and none of them are close calls:

- **Seven of thirteen custom objects carry no field table:** `AAO_Pair__c`, `AAO_Participant__c`, `AAO_Shadow_Person__c`, `AAO_Criterion__c`, `AAO_Flag__c` (deliberately, pointed elsewhere), `AAO_Run_Receipt__c`, `AAO_Created_Row__c`. **`AAO_Run_Receipt__c` and `AAO_Created_Row__c` do not appear under their API names anywhere in the document set**, and the create-leg object is live — MK-00000001 was born and purged through it.
- **`AAO_Pair__c` is the worst gap.** It is the atomic unit of the current pipeline — what call 1 emits, what resolution links, what verify disposes, what the join reads — and it has one paragraph describing three fields. `AAO_Pair__c.AAO_Disposition__c`, which design and CODE both worked on today, **appears nowhere in any document.**
- **The documented data flow describes a pipeline that no longer runs.** Model & Flow Part II is loop one and loop two, span check, blind re-check per loop. Call 0, the two comprehensive reads, the deterministic resolution stage, bucketed verify, the join and projection exist **only as prose in head stamps**, with no stage-to-object mapping anywhere. `AAO_Pair__c` never appears in any flow diagram.
- **No Apex class inventory and no charter-to-class map, anywhere.** The one table mapping steps to compute (Architecture, "What kind of code drives each step") names no classes and is two supersessions stale.
- **No ALTF field-level projection map.** Targets are named as objects and as English dimension names. Which `ALTF__Contact_Map_Details__c` field each dimension writes, and which values map to which, **exists only in `AAO_P8Codes` and in no document.**
- **Three of four custom metadata types are undocumented.** `AAO_Map_Value__mdt` and `AAO_People_Question__mdt` appear nowhere at all.
- **Built law lives outside the table that wins on fields.** `AAO_Answer__c.AAO_Support_Counter__c` is stated in a head stamp and absent from the Answer field table — which is precisely why design spent this afternoon reporting it did not exist.
- **The entity count disagrees with itself across the set:** sixteen in Architecture, fifteen in the Field Tables, a seventeenth proposed in the head, and none of those lists includes Pair, Criterion, Run Receipt or the create-leg record.

**2 · THIS IS THE ROOT OF THE DAY, and it is design's to own.** Three retractions in one session — the anchor fields, the stance controls, the sentiment counter — all have the same cause: **there is no current schema reference, so design read the org piecemeal and inferred the rest.** The documents were the defence against exactly that and they had rotted where it mattered. Matthew's correction stands: two years of decided architecture cannot be re-derived object by object in a chat window, and design should never have been proposing builds for things already built.

**3 · CODE OWNS FOUR DELIVERABLES, and each is from the repo or the org, never from memory.** Name the tree and the org id on every one, per the tree-and-org hazard.

- **(a) THE FLS-BLIND SCHEMA DUMP.** Every field on all thirteen custom objects and all four custom metadata types, read through the **Tooling API** so FLS cannot filter it: API name, type, length or precision, required, unique, external id, and **every picklist's full value set** — `AAO_Flag__c.AAO_Type__c` and `AAO_Cause__c`, `AAO_Pair__c.AAO_Disposition__c`, `AAO_Claim__c.AAO_Verdict_After__c` and `AAO_Subject_Type__c` and `AAO_Basis__c` and `AAO_Actor__c` and `AAO_Outcome__c`, `AAO_Answer__c.AAO_Verdict__c` and `AAO_Publication_State__c` and `AAO_Established_By__c`, `AAO_Shadow_Person__c.AAO_Reason__c` and `AAO_Identity_Provenance__c`. A file in the repo, not a chat report. Design will produce the same inventory through the standard API and the two get diffed — **any field design cannot see is an FLS gap and gets granted, since design's blindness this morning cost a day.**
- **(b) THE APEX INVENTORY.** Every class, one line each on what it does, and two maps: **class to charter** (People, Problems, Politics, Process, plus the resolver and the scope resolver) and **class to call** (call 0, the two comprehensive reads, call 2 resolution, call 3 verify, join, projection). Include the trigger classes and the validation rules — **the org taught CODE two of its own laws today** (a Located pair may not carry a person; a non-synthetic flag may never be deleted) and rules that teach us our own schema belong in the reference.
- **(c) THE CURRENT FLOW, STAGE BY STAGE, WITH THE OBJECT WRITTEN AT EACH STAGE.** One transcript from ingest to projection: admission, normalize, Source, Participant, call 0 and the roster, the two comprehensive reads, Pair, resolution and disposition, Candidate, verify, Claim, Answer, Criterion, Flag, Shadow Person, Created Row, Run Receipt, and the projection write with its watermark. This replaces loop one and loop two, which are retired and must be marked retired in place rather than deleted.
- **(d) THE ALTF PROJECTION MAP AT FIELD LEVEL, from `AAO_P8Codes`, not from prose.** For each dimension we project: the target `ALTF__` object and field API name, and the value-to-value mapping. Plus what we read and never write, by API name.

**4 · DESIGN'S HALF, running in parallel and not blocking CODE.** The org-side field inventory of all seventeen through the standard API, for the diff at (a); and then the fold — the deliverables become the rebuilt field tables and the rebuilt flow **inside Model & Flow, which the document set already says wins on fields**, with the Apex inventory and the ALTF map landing in Architecture. **Rebuild in place, not a new satellite:** a fifth living document would fragment the audit chain that this failure already proves is load-bearing. That placement is Matthew's to confirm.

**5 · WHY THIS COMES BEFORE THE RUNS, stated so it does not read as another delay.** The Wells Fargo read grades record state against expectations. **A read performed without a current field reference produces divergences nobody can classify** — the eighty-eighth stamp requires every divergence to be called finding, lawful fix, or authorship error, and that call cannot be made against a document set that does not know what `AAO_Pair__c` holds. This is not scope growth ahead of the test; it is the instrument the test grades with. **It is also mostly mechanical: the org has the truth and the repo has the code, and neither has to be invented.**

**6 · What this stamp does not change:** the read is otherwise cleared and nothing else blocks it — sentiment is built, the flag fires, the anchors are visible, `Held` is live, three sources are ingested. Production read-only unconditionally; LAW #1; existence versus visibility; three readings of one instrument class; the tree-and-org hazard; the liveness rule; the corrections law; every ruling of the eightieth through ninety-second stamps; human edits beat the machine forever.


## Ninety-second stamp · RETRACTION · SENTIMENT IS BUILT AND HAS BEEN SINCE JULY · `AAO_Answer__c.AAO_Support_Counter__c` is LIVE AND POPULATED on Project Farma and Emerson · the ninety-first's item 3 is VOID, the five rows grade normally, NOTHING IS OWED · design's third absence-from-an-incomplete-instrument error in one day

**Design session, 15 August, spliced above the ninety-first, never retyped. This stamp retracts a design finding that was wrong, names the error class, and clears the read to run. It carries no ruling and asks for none.**

**1 · THE RETRACTION.** Design reported that Support's counter had no field home in the org and escalated it to Matthew as a fork between building the counter and grading seven rows NOT MEASURABLE. **That was wrong.** `AAO_Answer__c.AAO_Support_Counter__c`, Number(2,0), exists and is populated. Two live specimens, read from `00DWD00000TWvH0YAL`'s org through the standard API:

```
Project Farma - Enterprise - 46 Seats   Adam Pfeiffer      counter 2   projected "Supporter"   TRUE   Transcript   occurred 2026-07-30
Emerson/Aspen Tech Insights 500         Neeraja Chimata    counter 2   projected "Supporter"   TRUE   Transcript   occurred 2026-06-17
```

Both sit at **+2, the top of Supporter** — which is the exact value the Charters record as reproducing Matthew's own grading of the first fixture through the ruled arithmetic. The mechanism is not owed, not thin, and not a proposal. It works, and it has since July.

**2 · THE ERROR, NAMED.** Design queried `AAO_Participant__c` and `AAO_Claim__c`, found no counter, and concluded the mechanism had no home. **It never queried `AAO_Answer__c`, which is where the projected per-person-per-scope state lives and always did.** This is the ninetieth stamp's own law broken by its own author, four hours after writing it: **an absence read from an instrument that cannot see the whole subject is not an absence.** Today's three instances, all design's: the anchor fields read absent through an FLS-filtered describe; the stance rows called uncontrolled because the corpus authors no flip; the counter called homeless because two of three objects were checked. **The law now reads in one more direction: name the objects you queried, or the absence is not established.**

**3 · WHAT IS VOID.** The ninety-first's item 3 is struck whole. There is no fork, no ruling owed from Matthew, and no build. **S4-02, S4-13, S4-15, S5-12 and ARC-3 grade normally**, and S4-07a and S4-12 grade on both halves, not one. `aao-wf-read-sheet-v0_3.csv` replaces the five `NONE IN INSTRUMENT` entries with their real liveness pairs: the counter's own movement is the positive control, and "stance HOLDS" means the counter moved while the band did not — an observable event, not an absence.

**4 · WHAT SURVIVES FROM THE NINETY-FIRST, unchanged.** The liveness rule itself: an absence row grades against a paired positive or it grades VACUOUS. The count: 36 of 46 rows carry an absence assertion. **S4-08 and S4-09 are graded together or not at all.** The rung 1/3 acceptance criterion. Those stand; only the counter finding was wrong.

**5 · THE READ IS CLEARED AND NOTHING BLOCKS IT.** Sentiment is built. The Identification flag is built and fires through the wired entry point. The anchor fields exist and are visible. `Held` is live. The three Wells Fargo sources are ingested with their occurred times. **Design has nothing further owed before the runs.** The sequence is Matthew's as ruled: session 3 alone first, look at what comes out against the expectations, then session 4 stacked and check that the counter incremented against prior state, that People and Problems carried forward, that the identity resolution held, then session 5. **Run it.**

**6 · A NOTE DESIGN OWES ITSELF.** Three retractions in one session is a pattern, not bad luck, and the pattern is design escalating an absence before establishing it. **The corrective is procedural, not attitudinal: before reporting that something does not exist, name every object and every API the search covered, and where the search is partial, report a partial search rather than an absence.** Matthew's correction stands on the record: this project has two years of decided architecture, and design's first move on a gap should be to read the org and the documents, never to propose a build.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; LAW #1; existence versus visibility; three readings of one instrument class; the tree-and-org hazard; the liveness rule; the counting laws; claim immutability; the corrections law; every ruling of the eightieth through ninety-first stamps not struck above; human edits beat the machine forever.


## Ninety-first stamp · THE READ SHEET GRADES ABSENCE IN 36 OF 46 ROWS, and absence is not evidence unless the mechanism could have fired · THE LIVENESS RULE · five rows have NO positive control anywhere in the Wells Fargo instrument · the wiring's definition of done is SEVEN ROWS, not a green suite

**Design session, 15 August, spliced above the ninetieth, never retyped. This stamp carries one new law for the read, one quantified finding against design's own instrument, and the acceptance criteria for the rung 1/3 wiring CODE is building now. It changes no ruling.**

**1 · THE COUNT, from `aao-wf-read-sheet-v0_2.csv`** (v0_1 plus two columns, `grades` and `liveness_pair`, nothing else altered): of 46 rows, **9 grade PRESENCE only, 14 grade ABSENCE only, 22 grade BOTH**, 1 is a mechanism check. **36 rows carry an absence assertion** — HOLDS, no second card, NO flag, establishes nothing, never sums, does not move, mints nothing, never resolved.

**2 · NEW LAW · THE LIVENESS RULE · A ROW THAT GRADES ABSENCE DOES NOT GRADE PASS ON ITS OWN.** Its verdict is conditional on a **liveness pair**: a row in the same run whose PRESENCE result proves that the mechanism it denies was capable of firing. **If the liveness pair fails, the absence row grades VACUOUS, never PASS.** A system whose components are unwired produces nothing very reliably, and nothing is what most of this sheet expects. Without this rule the Wells Fargo read can return a high pass rate that means almost nothing — the efficacy question answered by the instrument's own blind spot. Every row now names its pair in `liveness_pair`; where the pair is another run's row it is marked CROSS-RUN and cannot be graded from one run alone.

~~**3 · THE FINDING AGAINST DESIGN'S OWN INSTRUMENT · FIVE ROWS HAVE NO POSITIVE CONTROL ANYWHERE IN THE CORPUS.**~~ **[92nd: VOID. Design never queried `AAO_Answer__c`. `AAO_Support_Counter__c` is live and populated; the five rows grade normally and nothing is owed. See the ninety-second stamp.]** Nothing in s3, s4, s5 or the arc demonstrates that **a stance can move** — S4-02, S4-13, S5-12 and ARC-3 all assert a stance HOLDS, and no authored row anywhere shows a stance flipping. Nothing demonstrates that **an LOR grade can move DOWN** — S4-12 asserts a delivery-slip negative does not move Rohan down, and no row shows any grade moving down. **Those five are unfalsifiable within this instrument as written.** They are not defects in the system; they are a gap in the measuring device, and per the ledger's instrument law a gap in what the sheet can show becomes a gap in what the human can grade. Two lawful dispositions, and design does not pick unilaterally: take their liveness from the frozen graded regression corpus if it contains a stance flip and a down-move, and cite the specimen; or grade all five **VACUOUS** and say so in the result. **The corpus is frozen and authored — a stance flip cannot be added now without destroying the arc, so authoring one is not an option this weekend.**

**4 · THE ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA FOR THE RUNG 1/3 WIRING, and they are not a green suite.** Seven rows assert flag behaviour: S4-09 (rung 3 fires), S4-10, S5-15 and ARC-6 (one grouped flag), S4-11 (flag stays open), S5-01 (flag closes by identification), and **S4-08 (resolves silently, NO flag)**. On CODE's own report both raise sites live in `AAO_Resolve` and `AAO_Resolve` has **zero production callers**, so none of the seven can grade from a live run today. **The wiring is done when S4-09 fires on a live Wells Fargo run, not when the suite is green** — the suite already passes with the mechanism unreachable, which is the field-with-no-readers pattern in its sixth instance, a mechanism with no caller.

**5 · S4-08 IS THE SHARP END AND IT IS WHY THIS STAMP EXISTS.** S4-08 asserts a negative: Jared resolves and **no flag appears**. If the flag can never fire, S4-08 PASSES, and passes for exactly the wrong reason. S4-09 is its liveness pair; the two are graded **together or not at all**, and the combination S4-09 FAIL plus S4-08 PASS is diagnostic of an unwired flag, never a 1-of-2. This is the same failure design already caught once and fixed in one place — S5-19 exists because S5-01 could pass for the wrong reason, and S5-18 and DELTA-1 were rewritten as paired controls for the same reason. **The discipline was applied to three rows and never generalised. This stamp generalises it.**

**6 · WHAT IS RATIFIED FROM CODE'S IDENTIFICATION BUILD.** The three-value shape is right: `Identification` as the type with `Identity_Ambiguous` and `Identity_Unresolved` as causes, because picking between candidates and naming someone the org has never heard of are different work and one value would have collapsed them. Collect-at-both-sites rather than write is correct, and **the grep test that fails if `AAO_Resolve` ever gains a `raiseFor` is the structural version of the rule rather than the polite version** — the ledger's own doctrine, that where an instruction can fail you change the structure so the wrong answer cannot be expressed. The bound key, the never-reopen, and clear-by-`reconcile` with acknowledgement clearing nothing are all as ruled.

**7 · THE ORG FOUND A DEFECT IN OUR CODE AND THAT IS THE LEDGER PAYING OUT.** `AAO_Resolve.disposition()` never copies `AAO_About_Designator__c` forward — designator on the Located parent, person on the Identified child — so the clear query asking for both on one row could never have matched and the flag would never have cleared. **A hand-rolled in-memory fixture would have passed and shipped a flag that never clears.** CODE fixed the code and not the test. Recorded as an instance of evidence-over-inference including about our own schema, not as a new law: **fixtures go through the org's validation or they are not fixtures.**

**8 · TWO READERS WAITING ON WRITERS, both honest debt, neither graded by the read.** `AAO_Promoted_Contact__c` is read by the clear path and written by nothing — promotion is the eighty-eighth stamp's named debt, and reading it now means wiring promotion needs no change here. And the raise sites themselves, pending item 4. Both are named so neither is mistaken later for a working path.

Verified in-org by design: the Fatima map row retracted with its reason note ("A blank field cannot say that, so it is said here"), the other three Emerson rows untouched at their 21:27 stamps, the board at 9 cards with the CFO duplicate collapsed and the cross-type Goal/Obstacle pair standing untouched. Taken on report: the 20/7/10 standing counts, the 10 replacements plus 10 retirements, the byte-identical map diff, all timings and governors, suite 501 with 500 passing, and the commit.**

**1 · RATIFIED WHOLE, with the precedence named because it will recur: THE NUMBER IS TEN, NOT SEVEN.** The eightieth stamp said seven because the 13 August measurement tested identity; the ruling says intersection; CODE re-measured under THE RULE AS RULED and found ten, three of them exactly the wider-boundary shape design chose intersection for (9775-10048 against 9919-10048). **A RULING'S TEST OUTRANKS THE NUMBER ITS TRIGGERING MEASUREMENT PRODUCED**, and applying the rule rather than the stale number is credited, not merely accepted. Also ratified: the three-way branch in the sixty-ninth stamp's own priority order (completeness, then corroboration, then reinforcement); cross-Source needing no special case because offsets are per-artifact, which delivers the rejected option (b)'s intent free while keeping the three-presses-in-one-call signal, pinned by its own test; the card grain moving from equality to byte range scoped by type AND source, with the range claimed at write time so a one-batch pair cannot slip through; the non-suppression test protecting Matthew's cross-type grading specimen; the corrections run both-halves (replacement first, then retirement, claims being insert-only); and the DML-before-callout near-miss in the card writer, found by reading before overlap made it reachable, fixed with its reason in the comment.

**2 · TWO PHRASES OF DESIGN'S OWN, WRONG AT THE EIGHTIETH, CORRECTED IN PLACE THERE AND RECORDED HERE.** First: ~~Fatema is the acceptance case for the given-name rung~~. The rung matches exactly, "Fatima" and "Fatema" are one character apart, tried from the runtime, so the rung does not link her and never will without a fuzzy rung nobody has ruled; worse, while the twin stands the rung resolves "Fatima" TO THE TWIN, because an aggressive matcher finds a minted duplicate as readily as a real person. **WHAT CLOSED THE DEFECT IS THE CREATION BAR, and Fatema is the bar's acceptance case.** The rung's own acceptance case is a lone token matching a real given name, tested. Second: ~~the disowned Contact is enumerable for the sandbox purge exactly because the record carries it~~. `AAO_Purge` deletes machine-created Contacts only where NOT disowned, and that filter is the twenty-first stamp's ruling verbatim: disowned means we stop touching it, deletion included, the human deciding with facts we do not have. CODE reading the older ruling over design's newer phrase, and changing nothing on a hunch, is the receipt rule working. **THE TWIN'S DISPOSITION IS THEREFORE MATTHEW'S BY HAND**, item 5 below.

**3 · RULING (design under the delegated mode, veto open) · THE RESOLUTION DIVERGENCE CLOSES: OPTION (a), THE 27TH STAMP'S OWN SHAPE, PROPOSAL-FIRST.** The finding: `AAO_Resolve`, the deterministic resolution stage carrying the two-read merge, the designator ladder and the corroboration marker, has zero production callers; the pass drives `AAO_Pass.identify`, a model call over every located pair; 0 of 67 stacked-run pairs carry corroboration where r1q10 and r2q7 quote identical bytes with identical meaning and should have merged. The twenty-seventh stamp's resolution stage is WRITTEN LAW, ratified and gated in its era, and the driven path has diverged from it; this is the third law-versus-path divergence found by measurement (the coverage-Internal flag, the model-separation guard, now this), and the answer to the class is always the same: the path comes back to the law or the law is re-ruled in the open, never silent divergence. Option (b) is rejected because it leaves the merge as dead code that reads as live and corroboration as a field nothing writes, exactly when Matthew's confidence-merge lean at the thirty-third stamp wants that marker real. Option (c) is rejected because notes and emails carry no roster, the designator path is the ONLY path there, and retiring the deterministic stage would demolish the substrate of the channel this whole rebuild was aimed at. **THE CONDITIONS, because the pass's shape is being touched mid-era: PROPOSAL BEFORE WIRING.** (i) A per-leg diff report first: what `AAO_Pass.identify` actually does today, leg by leg, against the twenty-seventh stamp's text and against `AAO_Resolve`, because the gate-era evidence (234 ms deterministic, one model leg on the remainder) and today's measurement (a model call over every located pair) cannot both describe the current pass and the diff says which era drifted. (ii) ONE implementation survives, per the twenty-eighth stamp: after wiring, `AAO_Resolve` and `identify` reconcile into one stage with the deterministic legs in front and the model leg on the remainder only. (iii) Call 2's batch arithmetic and its grammar cap RE-MEASURE on the post-merge pair volume, the thirty-fifth stamp's cap-keyed-to-shape condition. (iv) The one-for-one ledger arithmetic restates across the merge: located, merged, disposed reconcile or the run does not count. (v) Measured before and after on the frozen fixtures: model calls, merged count, corroborated count, wall. The join-side backstop from the eightieth stamp stays regardless, because different-meaning pairs are meant to survive the merge and still collapse onto one answer. **SEQUENCE: this wiring lands BEFORE the Wells Fargo corpus runs and before the LOR measurement run, so both measure the lawful shape.**

**4 · THE CONFIG INVENTORY IS CONFIRMED, one line per item, and three statuses sharpened; the satellite moves to v0.2 beside this stamp.** The internal-domain list lives in `AAO_Seed.json`, one degree further from a config surface than "static resource" suggested. There is NO lane flag at all; batch versus express is today a property of how a driver sequences calls. And knob feature detection is TEMPERATURE-SPECIFIC, not general: the forty-third stamp's "feature detection now spans model capabilities generally" is corrected in place there per this runtime confirmation, marked not deleted; the BYO-LLM law stands and the generalization is owed, not built.

**5 · OPEN TO MATTHEW, his alone:** (a) **THE PLAIN-WORD YES ON `aao-politics-lor-draft-v1_0.md`**, now the single blocker on LOR: CODE lawfully parked the contracts because this ledger records no yes. (b) **THE TWIN**, Contact `003WD00001RCfLzYAL` "Fatima": delete it by hand in the sandbox (its reach, measured: 1 retracted map row, 2 card links, 1 participant, 1 answer) or accept it standing disowned. Recorded beside it: the signer's Signature Approver evidence is retired-held with sound words and a wrong subject, and the lawful door for landing it on the real Fatema Choudray is THE ALIAS, never a merge and never a fuzzy rung; a human alias plus a rerun re-attaches it, if he wants it landed. (c) Standing: the cross-type Goal/Obstacle card pair, his grade, the type-matching regression specimen.

**6 · CODE's queue, in order:** (a) the per-leg diff report per item 3(i), report only; (b) the wiring proposal, then the wiring on its ratification, conditions 3(ii) through 3(v); (c) on Matthew's yes, the LOR contracts and the read-only measurement run; (d) the WF corpus runs after both. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes, receipt standing.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; claim immutability; the counting laws; call 3's blindness; the eightieth stamp's rulings, which item 1 ratifies as built and items 2 corrects only in its two phrases; People closed; human edits beat the machine forever.

## Eightieth stamp · BOTH STRETCHES RATIFIED, the stacked run verified in-org · DESIGN'S OWN "THREE CALLS" CLAIM MARKED WRONG IN PLACE, and CODE's stop is the state-claim law working against its own author · RULING: REINFORCEMENT COUNTS DISTINCT UTTERANCES, byte intersection is the test, and it binds at BOTH grains · NEW DEFECT, found by design in-org: A DUPLICATE PERSON WAS CREATED AND LANDED AT THE TOP OF THE LADDER · one cross-type card pair goes to Matthew as the first real-speech calibration specimen

**Design session, 13 August, ratifying two CODE stretches (`90c23c6`, `a8b0a9e`). Organization queried first: aossb2 `00DWD00000DV7iT2AT`, IsSandbox true. Verified by design in-org: the four Emerson map rows and their notes and modstamps, the ten cards and their two creation batches, the card long descriptions and quotes, the created Contact record, and the absence of any Fatema map row. Taken on report: all timings, callouts, governors, the 20/17 reinforcement counts, the 7 same-byte pairs, suite 490 with 489 passing, and both commits.**

**1 · DESIGN'S ERROR, MARKED WRONG IN PLACE, NEVER DELETED.** The seventy-eighth and seventy-ninth stamps both say the Emerson stacked run is ~~"three frozen graded calls on one clean deal"~~. **WRONG ON BOTH COUNTS.** The freeze list carries two Emerson calls, one of which is `emerson/aspentech-2026-06-17-nf1`, THE TRAINING SET, and the third frozen fixture is Black and Veatch, a different account that cannot stack; the deal was also not clean, carrying 743 pairs, 66 claims and 17 standing answers. Design asserted a state claim without opening the freeze list, which is the exact law this project wrote for itself: A STATE CLAIM IS UNVERIFIED UNTIL THE DOCUMENT CARRYING IT IS OPENED BY EXACT PATH, IMMEDIATELY BEFORE IT IS ASSERTED. It failed in the ledger, in the enforcer's own hand, twice. **CODE MEASURED THE PREMISE BEFORE SPENDING THE RUN AND STOPPED, then ran the honest two-call version. CREDITED, and it is the discipline working in the direction that matters most, upward.** THE CONSEQUENCE STANDS AS CODE STATED IT: the stacked run is a MECHANISM result and carries NO accuracy reading, because half its evidence is the fixture everything was tuned against. Nothing in this stamp claims otherwise.

**2 · RATIFIED · the four owed items (`90c23c6`).** (a) The no-element mint guard, refusing at mint and naming the contract; the finding underneath it is kept as standing law because it generalizes: **before uphold-governs, a zero-element contract was caught only by accident, `isFull` returning false on an empty list, so every establishment against it read UNVERIFIED forever with no error and no name. A DEFECT IN THE QUESTION MUST NEVER BE PAID FOR BY THE EVIDENCE.** (b) The serial-driver caveat written into `plannedVerifyShards`'s own header with its measured numbers, which is the right home: a caveat in a report nobody rereads is not a caveat. (c) The comparison satellite with its caveat above the table. (d) THE LOR WRITE TRIED FROM THE RUNTIME rather than described: create and update both work, the probe row then removed because it carried no evidence, the create-leg record kept and disowned with its reason. **The capability law applied exactly as written, a describe is not a try, and the deletion of the probe row is the never-fabricate law reaching our own test scaffolding.** The last unverified capability in the LOR draft is closed.

**3 · RATIFIED · the two-call stacked run (`a8b0a9e`), and what it proved, verified in-org.** Per-call sections real: six cards created 21:28:54 under 17 June, four created 21:33:06 under 29 July. **THE MAP FIRMS UP OVER CALLS ON REAL SPEECH:** Jefferson Vargas and Ryan Couture both read `Multiple contacts` for appearing on both calls, Neeraja Chimata `Brief contact` for appearing on one, which is the thesis People closed on, now a property of a live map rather than an argument. Call 0 read DUAL on the 17 June fixture, which gate assertion 1 has wanted since the tenth stamp recorded it failing as OPPORTUNITY; the fourteenth stamp's one-way scope split is proven on the fixture that motivated it. The double call-0 flake stopping the run is the thirty-fifth stamp's retry policy behaving exactly as ruled, refusing to route on an unchecked scope. CODE's three driver errors are recorded as driver errors and not pipeline defects, each self-diagnosed and each fixed by regenerating from the untouched harvest: projection and cards in one transaction (the uncommitted-work fallback wrote raw verbatim faces, one card reading "No, I don't."), and both verify buckets in one transaction (callout after DML). **AND THE HUMAN-ROW QUESTION IS ANSWERED, NOT ASSUMED: no human-watermarked row was destroyed by this purge.** Design checked: Fatema Choudray's 7 August Inner Circle edit was already gone before this run, removed at Matthew's own direction at the forty-first stamp, and the forty-third stamp's in-org verification recorded it at the time in these words: "Emerson holds 0 rows per Matthew's condition."

**4 · RULING (design under the delegated mode, veto open) · REINFORCEMENT COUNTS DISTINCT UTTERANCES; THE TEST IS BYTE INTERSECTION WITHIN A SOURCE.** CODE's option (a), sharpened by evidence found in-org this session, and it is three existing laws completing themselves rather than a new idea: the sentiment counter already moves once per DISTINCT establishment; the sixty-second stamp already ruled that a rerun of the same words is not the buyer saying it twice; the sixty-ninth stamp already named COMPLETENESS, CORROBORATION and REINFORCEMENT as three axes that must never collapse into one another. **THE RULE: a claim reinforces only where its spans intersect NO span already standing on the answer, compared within the same Source. Any intersection, not only identity, and not only containment.** A claim that intersects counts UNCHANGED and is MARKED CORROBORATED, the twenty-seventh stamp's own marker finally doing work. **NOTHING IS DELETED AND NOTHING IS REFUSED: both claims stand as evidence with their own spans, the mixed-evidence law prices them as it always did, and only the COUNTER changes**, which is the whole point, because the counter is a number guidance orders by and an inflated one is a lie about how hard somebody pressed. Option (b), requiring a different occasion, is REJECTED with its reason recorded: a person who presses the same point three times in one call did press it three times, and that is real signal the rule must keep. Cross-Source comparison needs no special case: byte offsets are per-artifact, so claims from different calls never intersect and always count, which delivers (b)'s intent for free.

**Why INTERSECTION and not identity, and this is the part the in-org check bought:** the same defect is live at the CARD grain in a shape identity would miss. Two Obstacle cards stand on the 29 July call, both Jefferson Vargas, `a0pWD0000043HgMYAU` ("CFO approval blocks non-net-110 terms") and `a0pWD0000043HgNYAU` ("CFO rejects non-net-110 payment terms"), **and the second card's quote is a literal substring of the first card's quote.** One utterance, two reads, two boundaries, two cards on a board a customer would read. Identity would let it through; intersection catches it. **THE RULING THEREFORE BINDS AT BOTH GRAINS**, the answer counter and the card board's layer-1 identity key, which is the sixty-second stamp's own two-layer dedup extended from equal to overlapping. **THE EXISTING SEVEN inflated counts on Emerson, and this card pair, correct through the lawful path: retire and re-derive, never delete, never edit in place.**

**5 · NEW DEFECT, HIGH, found by design in-org and not present in any report · THE ASR-NAME HAZARD FIRED LIVE: A DUPLICATE PERSON WAS CREATED AND WROTE THE TOP OF THE LADDER ONTO A CUSTOMER-NAMED MAP.** Contact `003WD00001RCfLzYAL` was created 2026-08-13T21:32:41Z with LastName "Fatima", NO first name, no email, no title, on Emerson Electric Co., and her map row `a0eWD000003oRZZYA2` reads `Signature Approver`, rank 5, cited to Ryan Couture on 29 July. **THE SEEDED CONTACT `003WD00001PsSLIYA3`, Fatema Choudray, VP Premier Sales Performance, ON THE SAME ACCOUNT, IS THE SAME HUMAN.** The twenty-first stamp named this hazard in advance and in almost these words: a mentioned name is what a transcriber heard, and a Contact minted from a transcript spelling carries that error into the CRM permanently. The twenty-second stamp's table called buq1 "Fatima" the ONE CLEAN CASE, the row the candidate-set widening exists to LINK. It created instead. **TWO MECHANISMS FAILED AND BOTH ARE NAMEABLE.** The ladder has no rung that matches a bare given name against the account, so exact-name matching returned zero and ladder-zero reached creation. And the twenty-fourth stamp's free structural guard did not hold: its reasoning was that a Contact requires a LastName so "CFO" can never create, which is true of titles and NOT true of a single-token first name, because one token parses into LastName and satisfies the schema. The guard was believed to mean only a real full name can create; it never meant that.

**THE RULING (design under the delegated mode, veto open), and it is Matthew's own law made mechanical rather than a new decision. MATCH AGGRESSIVELY: the resolver gains a GIVEN-NAME RUNG, a bare single-token designator matched against given names scoped to the account and the deal's map rows, the ladder's standard three outcomes unchanged, exactly one match LINKS, more than one is AMBIGUOUS and holds, zero falls through. CREATE CONSERVATIVELY: A SINGLE-TOKEN DESIGNATOR CAN LINK BUT NEVER CREATE**, completing the twenty-fourth stamp's guard by its own logic, so ladder-zero on one token is HELD with its evidence and the Identification flag, never minted. Both are deterministic, additive, and touch no charter text and no model call. **THE CORRECTION, lawful path only:** the Fatima map row and its claims retire in place with their reason, the establishment re-attaches to the real Contact if and only if the rebuilt ladder links it, and THE CREATED CONTACT IS MARKED AND DISOWNED IN THE CREATE-LEG RECORD, never deleted by us as product law, and enumerable for the sandbox purge exactly because the record carries it. *(Eighty-first stamp: the second clause is WRONG and corrected in place. Disowning EXCLUDES a row from the purge, per the twenty-first stamp's own semantics that the code carries verbatim: disowned means we stop touching it, deletion included. The twin's disposition is Matthew's by hand.)* **Fatema becomes the acceptance case for the given-name rung, standing beside buq2/Pat for the internal check.** *(Eighty-first stamp: HALF WRONG, corrected in place. The rung matches exactly and does not link Fatima to Fatema, and while the twin stands it resolves to the twin; what closed the defect is the CREATION BAR, and Fatema is the bar's acceptance case, not the rung's.)* Recorded beside it, not a defect: never-blank wrote rank 5 from one third-party mention, which is the rule behaving as ruled, and it is why the identity leg in front of it has to be right.

**6 · OPEN TO MATTHEW, one card pair, the first cross-type calibration specimen on real speech.** Cards `a0pWD0000043An0YAE` (Obstacle) and `a0pWD0000043An1YAE` (Goal) carry IDENTICAL faces, identical descriptions and the identical quote from Neeraja Chimata: "we want to get ahead of the relationship map gap that we have for sales". The Problems draft's own guidance says a want is a Goal and a thing in the way is an Obstacle, so on its face the Goal is right and the Obstacle is a type error, and cross-type confusion is the calibration target that draft named. **Design does not grade it. Matthew reads both cards on the board and rules, and his grade becomes the type-matching regression specimen.** The card grain also means the two are not duplicates by construction, which is why this is a grading question and not the item 4 defect wearing another coat.

**7 · CODE's queue, in order:** (a) the reinforcement ruling per item 4, both grains, with the seven Emerson counts and the CFO card pair corrected through the lawful path and reported by rows; (b) the identity ruling per item 5, the given-name rung and the single-token creation bar, with the Fatima row corrected and Fatema as its acceptance case; (c) then the LOR contracts and the read-only measurement run, on Matthew's plain-word yes on `aao-politics-lor-draft-v1_0.md`, which item 2(d) has now unblocked on capability; (d) the config-inventory status confirmation, one line per item, riding any report. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes, receipt standing.

**8 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; claim immutability; the counting laws, and specifically that NOTHING in the stacked run counts toward accuracy; call 3's blindness; the merge key at verify, which item 4 deliberately does not touch; People closed; human edits beat the machine forever.

**3 · LOR DISCOVERY, run this session per the seed, evidence over inference. Matthew's direction, his words in substance: LOR enters Politics as an output and tracking mechanism, for real relationship management valid up and across the organization and over time; net new to our idea of People.** `ALTF__LOR_Relationship__c`, identical schema in both orgs: `ALTF__Contact__c` (required, to Contact), `ALTF__User__c` (to User, NULLABLE), `ALTF__CurrentLOR__c` picklist with stored values `Unknown, Vendor, Credible Source, Problem Solver, Trusted Advisor` and display labels carrying the ranks ("0. **4 · THE DESIGN FACTS THAT BIND, stated before any proposal.** (a) The junction is PAIR-GRAIN, seller times customer person, and DEAL-AGNOSTIC: evidence will arrive on deal calls, the output is cross-deal by the vendor's own model, and the Claim's four keys already record account grain for the later roll-up, so nothing new is needed to keep history honest. THE PAIR-ATTRIBUTION RULE rides whichever he picks: an establishment binds a specific seller only where the words bind one, and an unbindable establishment is HELD with its evidence, the identification-flag pattern, never guessed.

## Seventy-seventh stamp · DO THIS FIRST, NOTHING ELSE UNTIL IT IS DONE · the complete never-blank decision procedure, no case left open, no question to ask back · plus the LABEL-TO-VALUE MAPPING configured on the People charter

**Execution session. Matthew, 13 August, plainly: fix the blank projections now, choose a rule and go, he does not care which of most-likely, highest, or most-conservative as long as it moves. Design chooses below so CODE never has to ask. This stamp supersedes the ordering of every queue item that precedes it; it is first.**

**1 · THE PROCEDURE, complete, deterministic, and exhaustive. No branch of it ends in a blank where anything is established.** For a dimension on a person, at projection:
- Take the set of ESTABLISHED values (upheld, per the sixty-ninth stamp's uphold-governs rule).
- If the set is EMPTY: write nothing and keep the refusal note. This is the only path that leaves a field unwritten.
- If the set has ONE value: WRITE IT. No hold, no note-instead-of-value, no exception.
- If the set has MORE THAN ONE: WRITE THE HIGHEST-RANKED. Every unwritten established value rides the Option C note with its citation, phrased as evidence that also indicates it.
- If a value carries NO RANK in the seed (an org's custom or relabelled entry): it sorts BELOW every ranked value but remains eligible; if the established set contains only unranked values, write the FIRST BY THE VENDOR PICKLIST ORDER. An unranked value never causes a blank.
- A HUMAN-SET VALUE IS NEVER OVERWRITTEN by any branch above; that law is untouched and outranks all of it.
**Ranks, buyer role, keyed to stored value:** 5 `Signature Approver`, 4 `Decision Maker`, 3 `Approver`, 2 `Evaluator`, 1 `User`, 0 `Unknown`. **Political status:** 3 `Inner Circle`, 2 `Political Structure`, 1 `Outside Political Structure`, 0 `Unknown`. **Support and sentiment are excluded** and keep their counter arithmetic, net zero reading Neutral per the forty-fourth stamp.

**2 · RULING (Matthew's) · THE LABEL-TO-VALUE MAP IS CONFIGURATION ON THE PEOPLE CHARTER.** Because an org may relabel a slot (production displays `Signature Approver` as "Decision Maker and Approver", evidenced at the seventy-sixth stamp), the People charter carries a configurable map from the org's DISPLAYED label to the stored value it processes as, alongside the rank per stored value. Ships as our seed with the defaults above, org-overridable, read per org per run like every other org fact. The code continues to read and write STORED VALUES only; the map exists so an org whose words differ can state what its words mean, rather than having our ranking silently mean something else on its map. No label string is ever hardcoded in logic, and a test asserts it.

**3 · CODE builds, in this order, and reports nothing else until these land:** (a) the procedure in item 1 for Buyer Role and Political Status; (b) the ranks and the label map as seed metadata per item 2; (c) re-project the resident Project Farma run so the board shows the result — projection alone if it can run without a fresh pass, and if it cannot, say so and run what is needed. ACCEPTANCE, on the board: Adam Pfeiffer's Buyer Role reads `Approver` with Evaluator cited in his note, and no person on the deal carries a blank dimension where evidence exists.

**4 · Everything else waits:** the no-element contract guard, the serial-driver caveat, the machine-versus-human satellite, the second call, the Emerson stacked run. They are not cancelled, they are behind this.

**5 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; claim immutability; call 3's blindness; People closed; human edits beat the machine forever.

## Seventy-sixth stamp · THE FULL BUYER-ROLE LADDER IS RULED, keyed to STORED VALUES never labels · and the org-label divergence is EVIDENCED IN BOTH ORGS: the same stored value reads "Signature Approver" in the sandbox and "Decision Maker and Approver" in production · the ranking ships as OVERRIDABLE SEED METADATA

**Execution session. Matthew read the production picklist in the vendor UI and asked whether the data differs or only the label. Design checked both orgs by describe this session; the answer is decisive and it closes the seventy-fifth stamp's open item.**

**1 · MEASURED, both orgs: THE PICKLIST VALUES ARE IDENTICAL.** `ALTF__Contact_Map_Details__c.ALTF__Buyer_Role__c` carries exactly six values in production (`00DHn000006jYatMAE`) and in the sandbox (`00DWD00000DV7iT2AT`): `Approver`, `Decision Maker`, `Evaluator`, `User`, `Signature Approver`, `Unknown`. Production's relationship map displays six options (DA, D, A, E, U, ?), six for six, so **"Decision Maker and Approver" is the DISPLAY LABEL for the stored value `Signature Approver`.** The data did not change; the word did. `ALTF__Translation__c` was checked and does not carry it (it serves questions, qualifiers and quicklinks), so the relabel lives in the vendor's own display layer, which is theirs and not ours to read.

**2 · THE HAZARD THIS EXPOSES, named because it generalizes:** the same stored value reads "Signature Approver" in one org and "Decision Maker and Approver" in another, and THOSE ARE NOT SYNONYMS — one names the person who signs, the other names a person who both decides and approves. So a ladder keyed to labels would rank different things in different orgs, and a ladder keyed to values ranks one slot whose meaning an org can move under us. This is the eighty-percent hazard in miniature, met on our own two orgs before a single pilot.

**3 · RULING (Matthew's) · THE LADDER, his numbering, keyed to STORED VALUES:** 5 `Signature Approver` (production displays "Decision Maker and Approver"), 4 `Decision Maker`, 3 `Approver`, 2 `Evaluator`, 1 `User`, 0 `Unknown` and blank. The highest-ranked ESTABLISHED value projects; every unprojected established role rides the note with its citation, stated as evidence that also indicates it. Combined with the seventy-fifth stamp's never-blank rule: where anything is established a value is always written, and where the ladder cannot rank a set the lowest established value is written rather than nothing.

**4 · RULING (design under the delegated mode, veto open, and it follows LAW #1's own pattern) · THE RANKING SHIPS AS SEED METADATA, ORG-OVERRIDABLE, KEYED TO API VALUE, NEVER TO LABEL.** The code reads stored values, which is permitted and necessary and is not a dependency; the rank per value ships as our seed and an org may override it, exactly as the ontology does. An org that relabels its top slot can re-rank it without a code change, and no future session hardcodes a label anywhere. Adam Pfeiffer's row is the acceptance case: Approver plus Evaluator established, `Approver` projects at rank 3, Evaluator rides the note with its citation, the field is never blank.

**5 · CODE's queue, replacing the seventy-fifth stamp's item 5:** (a) never-blank across Buyer Role and Political Status per the seventy-fifth stamp's items 2 and 4b, with the lowest-established or most-clear fallback; (b) the five-value ladder per items 3 and 4 above, as overridable seed metadata keyed to stored values, with a test asserting no label string appears in the ranking; (c) re-project Project Farma's resident run so Adam's row fills without a fresh pass if the projection can run alone; then the standing items. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes standing.

**6 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally, and this session's reads of it were describes only; LAW #1, which this stamp applies rather than amends; the receipt rule; claim immutability; call 3's blindness; People closed; human edits beat the machine forever.

The unprojected value rides the note with its citation exactly as buyer role does. Blank survives only where nothing is established. Call 3 and the card-face call use a keyed object — one property per unit, every key required, the envelope enforced structurally — and neither has this failure. **5 · STANDING LAW, generalized from this because it will recur: KEYED GRAIN NEEDS A KEYED SCHEMA. Prose is not enforcement.** Where a call must return exactly one unit per input unit, the structure carries that requirement or nothing does — an array plus a description is a request, and a model may satisfy it, degenerate, or duplicate, with no failure the schema can catch. Every future keyed-grain call is built keyed. This is the evidence-over-inference law applied to our own prompts.

**2 · RATIFIED · THE CITATION DEFECT, and it is the integrity condition earning its place immediately.** The citation paired `spans[0].speaker` with the ANSWER'S occurred clock, which only moves forward, so an establishment carrying March's and June's words credited March's speaker with June's date: A CITATION FOR A MOMENT THAT NEVER HAPPENED. Harmless on today's single-source data, which is precisely why it survived unseen. Every span now carries the date it was said. The seventieth stamp's integrity condition found a real defect within hours of being written.

**3 · RATIFIED · the uphold governs, 4 of 22 proposals change** (`r2q6`, `r1q9`, `r2q33`, `r2q32`), three on single-element contracts where Partial was never expressible.

**4 · CONSEQUENCE ONE, design's miss, correctly resolved: A COMPLETING UTTERANCE WAS BEING COUNTED AS REINFORCEMENT.** The branch was unreachable while partials wrote UNVERIFIED (the verdicts differed), so the ruling that unblocked truth also unblocked a conflation — collapsing two of the three axes the sixty-ninth stamp explicitly forbade collapsing, into a count that guidance orders by. RATIFIED AS FIXED: a claim carrying a previously-missing element is ESTABLISHED, not Reinforced. Completeness rose; nobody said it twice.

**5 · CONSEQUENCE TWO, the serious one, and CODE stopping on it is the catch of the week. THE RULING WAS ABOUT TO REVERSE A HELD DECISION ON A CUSTOMER-VISIBLE OBJECT.** The criteria gate read the naming answer's VERDICT, which only worked because a partial used to write UNVERIFIED. Under the new ruling, partial criteria would have begun reaching `ALTF__Decision_Criteria__c` — silently reversing "whole criteria only, partials' fate is Matthew's, held". NEITHER THE SIXTY-NINTH NOR THE SEVENTIETH STAMP MENTIONS CRITERIA, so design ruled a verdict law without tracing who was reading verdicts as a proxy for something else. The gate now reads COMPLETENESS, which is what it always meant, and that forced the other half of the sixty-eighth stamp's self-contradiction closed: the join stamped every element covered unconditionally, so completeness was always "complete" and the gate would have been decorative. THE STANDING LESSON, added to the rulings law: A RULING THAT CHANGES WHAT A FIELD MEANS MUST NAME EVERY GATE THAT READS THAT FIELD, because a proxy reader is silently re-ruled by a change nobody aimed at it. Matthew's hold on partial criteria STANDS, untouched.

**6 · RATIFIED · the rename**, three objects, 19 classes, zero old references, old field retired in place rather than deleted because deleting destroys its data, 41 answers and 128 candidates backfilled, and 104 CLAIMS DELIBERATELY NOT backfilled because claims are insert-only and their history stays where it was written. Nine tests corrected in place, none deleted; `claimsCannotBeEdited` looked alarming and was a no-op write on an already-TRUE verdict, immutability intact, checked rather than assumed.

Elements are how completeness is expressed; a contract without them cannot express it and is a mint-time data defect. The guard goes at mint: refuse, name the contract, no silent default. This is a small build and it is CODE's next stretch's first item.

Design recommends doing it before any demo so the board Matthew shows matches the law the ledger carries; CODE spends nothing until he says.
