## Seventieth stamp · MATTHEW OVERRULES DESIGN: ELEMENTS DO ACCUMULATE ACROSS UTTERANCES, one plus one is two · and the accumulation is a JOIN-SIDE DETERMINISTIC UNION, so no model reads state and call 3 stays blind · the open-questions register is written

**Execution session. Matthew rejected the sixty-ninth stamp's item 4 and he is right. Corrected in place below, and the mechanism that makes his ruling safe is named, because his own stated worry (the model would have to read current state every time) has a better answer than the one his question assumed.**

**1 · THE SIXTY-NINTH STAMP'S ITEM 4 IS WRONG WHERE IT STANDS.** It ruled that elements never union across utterances, on the ground that assembling March's element with June's manufactures a statement nobody made. That reasoning conflates ACCUMULATION with PRESENTATION. The person did say both things; both are real, both are byte-located, both are dated and attributable. The fabrication risk lives entirely in showing them as one continuous sentence, not in counting them both.

**2 · RULING (Matthew's) · COMPLETENESS ACCUMULATES ACROSS UTTERANCES.** If March's words satisfy one element and June's different-but-related words satisfy the missing one, the requirement is complete and the value reads complete. The gate is met by the second statement, and the establishment stands as fully evidenced.

**3 · THE MECHANISM, and it costs nothing Matthew feared · THE UNION IS DETERMINISTIC AND HAPPENS AT THE JOIN, NOT IN A MODEL.** Matthew's worry was that this forces the model to read current state on every pass. It does not. The reads and the verifier stay stateless and blind: call 1 proposes what is in the transcript in front of it, call 3 judges the cited words alone, and NEITHER IS EVER TOLD WHAT MARCH ESTABLISHED — call 3's blindness is untouched, which matters because it is the law that makes every rejection count worth anything. The join already reads the existing answer before branching (the read-then-branch shape bulkified at the forty-eighth stamp), so the accumulation is SET ARITHMETIC ON ELEMENT IDS in code: union the new establishment's covered elements with the standing answer's, recompute completeness, done. No new model call, no new tokens, no new flux, no state in the prompt.

**4 · THE INTEGRITY CONDITION, which is what makes it honest.** Every element keeps ITS OWN span: verbatim, byte offsets, speaker, and the date it was said. The answer's span array already holds multiple dated spans (verified in-org: a single answer carrying four spans at different offsets), so the receipt is already a list and not a merged sentence. THE LAW: an accumulated establishment is NEVER rendered as one continuous quote. It renders as its elements, each with its own words and date ("condition named, 3 March, Adam Pfeiffer; tied to the decision, 12 June, Adam Pfeiffer"). Same subject, same contract, always. A reader must be able to see that this was assembled over time, and when it was said. Cross-source is fine and expected; cross-subject is never.

**5 · Why the risk is small in practice, stated so nobody over-worries it:** under Matthew's ruling at the sixty-ninth stamp the uphold already governs truth, so completeness no longer gates anything — the union improves a QUALITY READING, it cannot manufacture a TRUE that the verifier did not grant. And on today's fixture no genuinely partial establishment exists at all.

**6 · CODE's queue, replacing the sixty-ninth stamp's item 6(b):** implement the join-side union per items 3 and 4 — union covered element ids on the standing answer, retain every element's own dated span, never merge spans into one quote, same subject and contract only; report by rows what changes on the current fixture (expected: nothing, since nothing is genuinely partial there). Everything else in that queue stands: derive-from-elements, the rename to completeness, the card-writer receipt leg.

**7 · THE OPEN-QUESTIONS REGISTER, at Matthew's direction:** everything not yet fully decided is now written in one plain-English place, `aao-open-questions.md`, beside this ledger. His instruction, recorded as standing practice: it is fine for the scope to carry parts marked temporary or undecided, provided they are NAMED as such in one place rather than scattered, so the whole can be hypothesized against and taken apart deliberately. The register is a satellite; the ledger stays the authority on what is ruled.

**8 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; the counting laws; CALL 3'S BLINDNESS, which this stamp deliberately protects; People closed; the sixty-ninth stamp's items 1, 3, and 5, which stand.

## Sixty-ninth stamp · MATTHEW RULES (a): THE UPHOLD GOVERNS · *(SEVENTIETH STAMP: ITEM 4 IS WRONG WHERE IT STANDS — elements DO accumulate across utterances; the union is deterministic at the join, with every element keeping its own dated span. Marked, not deleted.)* · and his three questions settle three more things: NO NEW STATE READ, ELEMENTS DO NOT UNION ACROSS UTTERANCES, and OUR "COVERAGE" IS RENAMED because the vendor owns that word · plus the finding that no genuinely partial establishment exists on this fixture at all

**Execution session recording Matthew's ruling and the design answers his questions force. His ruling: option (a), the uphold governs.**

**1 · RULING (Matthew's) · THE UPHOLD GOVERNS.** Where the blind verifier upheld the words, the answer reads TRUE; element completeness is recorded as data, never as a verdict gate. The read-side label stops deciding truth. This restores the layering the architecture was built on: call 1 finds, call 3 judges, and no read-side hedge outranks the verifier. Existing law backs it (verification sheet check 3: "Partial-coverage pairs accrue and displace nothing").

**2 · THE FINDING THAT SHRINKS THE PROBLEM, verified in-org this session:** there is currently NO genuinely partial establishment on Project Farma. The single multi-element answer (`Criteria · naming`, 3 elements) reads UNVERIFIED while its own element record says `missing: [], covered: [e1,e2,e3]` — complete. Every observed UNVERIFIED is the voiced hedge contradicting complete element data, so CODE's derive-from-elements fix resolves 100 percent of observed cases and the partial-coverage rules below govern a case this fixture has not yet produced. Recorded so nobody mistakes a hypothetical for a measurement.

**3 · Matthew's first question, answered: (a) REQUIRES NO NEW STATE READ.** The join already reads existing answers before branching (the read-then-branch shape CODE bulkified at the forty-eighth stamp), so current state is read today and (a) reads no more than that. It reads LESS logic, not more: the verdict stops depending on a second, model-voiced field.

**4 · Matthew's second question, RULED · ELEMENTS DO NOT UNION ACROSS SEPARATE UTTERANCES. Partial plus partial never equals full.** An establishment assembled from one element spoken in March and another in August is a statement nobody ever made, and its receipt would read as a coherent sentence that was never uttered — the exact fabrication the whole architecture exists to prevent. Within ONE idea spanning adjacent turns, stitching is already ruled and bounded by the span set (twenty-ninth stamp, one to three spans, byte-located, caps at the thirty-sixth). Across calls, a later utterance carrying all elements is a NEW and fuller establishment that reinforces the earlier one; it does not retroactively complete it. CODE confirms current join behaviour against this rule by rows and reports; if the join today unions covered elements across claims, that is a defect to fix, not a feature to keep.

**5 · Matthew's third question, RULED IN TWO PARTS.** **(i) THE WORD IS RENAMED.** "Coverage" is the vendor's word on the relationship map for how well a person is known (Brief contact, Multiple contacts, In-depth), and reusing it for how much of a question the words answer is a vocabulary collision on the one surface sellers actually read. OURS RENAMES: element COMPLETENESS, with `AAO_Element_Coverage__c` retiring to `AAO_Element_Completeness__c` and every document following. The Glossary rules the rename at the next fold, one term one meaning; the vendor's word is untouched because it is theirs. **(ii) WHAT MOVES COMPLETENESS TO FULL, and what does not.** Only WORDS THAT CARRY THE MISSING ELEMENT, from the same subject against the same contract, in one coherent statement. NOT repetition, NOT sentiment, NOT a second source. Those are three different axes that all feel like "more evidence" and mean different things, and the standing law now names them apart: COMPLETENESS is which parts of the question these words answer; CORROBORATION is whether both reads found it (a marker, never a gate, twenty-seventh stamp); REINFORCEMENT is how many times a person said it (the card counter). Collapsing any of them into the others is how a completeness measure silently becomes a confidence score, which nobody ruled and nobody could audit.

**6 · CODE's queue: (a)** the derive-from-elements fix already ordered at the sixty-eighth, now under this ruling: an upheld pair's answer reads TRUE regardless of the voiced label, with the count of changed proposals reported; **(b)** confirm by rows whether the join unions elements across claims, per item 4; **(c)** the rename per item 5(i), field and code, reported before any document edit so the Glossary fold absorbs one consistent change; **(d)** the standing unwired card-writer receipt leg. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes standing.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; the counting laws; call 3's blindness; People closed; the span set's caps; the card-versus-answer grain question, still open and still Matthew's.

## Sixty-eighth stamp · THE UNVERIFIED ANSWER IS DIAGNOSED AND DESIGN'S HYPOTHESIS WAS WRONG: A READ-SIDE COVERAGE HEDGE OVERRIDES THE BLIND VERIFIER'S UPHOLD · the receipt object is BUILT (commit 6fa72d2) and its seeded row deleted rather than shown · one ruling to Matthew

**Execution session ratifying CODE's diagnosis and the receipt build. Design's sixty-seventh-stamp guess (a sibling pair without a verdict) is marked WRONG where it stands: there is no sibling. The cause is upstream and structural.**

**1 · THE DIAGNOSIS, CODE's, by rows.** `AAO_PairCommit.cls:524` proposes the answer's verdict as `located.AAO_Coverage__c == 'Full' ? 'TRUE' : 'UNVERIFIED'`. `AAO_Coverage__c` is CALL 1's OWN COVERAGE LABEL, voiced by the read. So a read-side hedge decides the verdict, and A LATER UPHOLD BY THE BLIND VERIFIER DOES NOT OVERTURN IT. Dan's Obstacle claim was born UNVERIFIED with `verdict_before` null and FULL element coverage; no clobber, no monotonicity violation, the answer machinery correct throughout. Two such answers exist on the deal, not one (the second is Adam's `AAO_DC_N`).

**2 · THE SHARP PART, and it indicts design's own instruction text.** `AAO_PB_OBST` declares EXACTLY ONE ELEMENT, so "some of it" is not expressible: either the element is covered or there is no pair. Yet 8 of 51 coverage-taking pairs on single-element contracts returned Partial, a hedge design's own charter wording invites ("PARTIAL IS THE DEFAULT UNDER DOUBT"). And the candidate row CONTRADICTS ITSELF: it stamps `covered = [all elements], missing = []` while proposing UNVERIFIED from a different field. A row that disagrees with itself is a defect regardless of any ruling, so THE SELF-CONTRADICTION IS CODE'S TO FIX: coverage is DERIVED from element coverage (missing empty means Full), never taken from a separately voiced label, which removes the incoherence on single-element contracts entirely and needs no ruling. Why it looked arbitrary is worth keeping: Adam voiced four Obstacles, three proposing TRUE, so his answer accrued TRUE; Dan voiced one that proposed UNVERIFIED. Two identically-shaped establishments landing differently because one person said more things.

**3 · OPEN TO MATTHEW, ONE RULING, and existing law leans one way.** When a pair's coverage is genuinely partial on a MULTI-element contract and the blind verifier UPHELD it, what does the answer read? The verification sheet's check 3 already says "Partial-coverage pairs accrue and displace nothing (no TRUE downgraded by a later partial)", and call 3 is the project's verification authority while coverage measures completeness, not truth. **(a) THE UPHOLD GOVERNS** — the answer reads TRUE and partial-ness is recorded as element coverage, which is what element coverage is for; costs: an establishment can stand on incomplete element coverage, visible on the Inspector but no longer gating. **(b) COVERAGE GATES** — today's behaviour kept deliberately, the answer reads UNVERIFIED until an establishment covers every element; costs: the blind verifier is overridden by a read-side judgment, and the Inspector will keep showing upheld words whose answer says unverified. Design's lean is (a), on the standing law and on which layer is the authority. Matthew's call; nothing builds on it until he rules. THE CARD-VERSUS-ANSWER QUESTION AT THE SIXTY-SEVENTH STAMP STANDS UNCHANGED beside this and is not dissolved by it.

**4 · THE RECEIPT OBJECT, RATIFIED AS BUILT.** `AAO_Run_Receipt__c` plus `AAO_Receipt`, one row per Source per pass, legs merged per stage across transactions, wired into call 0, both reads, resolution, call 3, join and projection through new overloads so no existing signature moved; purge takes it with the run. Three decisions carry their reasons in code: TELEMETRY IS NEVER EVIDENCE (every entry point swallows its own failures, because a lost timing is small and a lost seven-callout run is not); limits read on the first line so a leg carries its stage's true consumption; WARM anywhere makes the pass WARM. CREDITED, and it is the day's third instance of the same discipline: CODE seeded a receipt to see the panel, then DELETED IT, because those wall times were typed rather than journalled and leaving them would have put a fabricated measurement on a real run key. No real pass has written a receipt yet; the first arrives with the next run.

**5 · CODE's error-policy self-report, recorded because it is a real cost honestly named:** the swallow-everything telemetry policy hid a defect for a whole build (the merge query omitted the external id, the upsert threw, the swallow ate it, every second stage silently replaced the first), caught only by tests asserting on row contents since the call returns null by design. The trade stands; the cost is now written down, and the standing consequence is that TELEMETRY WRITE PATHS ARE TESTED ON THEIR ROWS, never on their return.

**6 · CODE's queue: (a)** the coverage self-contradiction fix per item 2, derived not voiced, reported with the count of pairs whose proposal changes; **(b)** Matthew's ruling from item 3 when given; **(c)** owed and named by CODE: the card writer's receipt leg is unwired, and split events are always zero because no caller reports one yet. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes standing — and the first real receipt ships with the next run.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; the counting laws; call 3's blindness; People closed; the forty-ninth stamp's grain ruling.

The diagnosis was inference presented as evidence. Marked, not deleted.)* · two defects diagnosed, one of them real

**Execution session. Matthew placed `aaoRunInspector` on the Project Farma Opportunity page. It rendered, showed "no runs have touched this opportunity yet", and threw a component error. Design diagnosed both in the sandbox this session; CODE's own owed item ("nobody has seen it render") is what caught them, and the five minutes paid for themselves.**

**1 · DEFECT ONE, THE RUN LOOKUP, root cause proven.** The runs exist and are reachable; the controller's join is wrong. Measured this session:
- `AAO_Claim__c` CARRIES NO RUN KEY (verified by field read: it has `AAO_Opportunity__c` but no `AAO_Run_Key__c`), so any run list built from claims returns nothing.
- `AAO_Pair__c` carries `AAO_Run_Key__c` but NO opportunity field; it reaches the deal through `AAO_Source__c`.
- THE WORKING JOIN, run and verified: `SELECT AAO_Run_Key__c, COUNT(Id) FROM AAO_Pair__c WHERE AAO_Source__r.AAO_Opportunity__c = :opportunityId GROUP BY AAO_Run_Key__c` returns three runs on Project Farma: `pf0811-goal` (120 pairs), `pf0808-i1` (90), `pf0808-h1` (82). CODE uses this join, or an equivalent it proves, and the run picker fills.

**2 · DEFECT TWO, THE NULL GUARD.** `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'stages')` at `markup://c:aaoRunInspector`: the JS reads `stages` off a selected run that does not exist when the run list is empty. The empty case must render the empty state and nothing else. Note the honest consequence: an empty-state message and a thrown error appeared TOGETHER, which is how a surface tells you it does not know what it is doing; the guard makes the empty case truthful.

**3 · Standing law reaffirmed by this: A CAPABILITY IS UNVERIFIED UNTIL TRIED FROM THE RUNTIME THAT WILL MAKE THE CALL — and for UI, that runtime is the browser.** Controller-proven is not rendered-proven. Every future LWC is opened on a page before its stretch reports done; the Inspector's own verification is not complete until Matthew sees the walk-back on screen.

**4 · CODE's queue: (a)** fix both defects; **(b)** RENDER IT and report what was seen, including the run picker listing all three runs and one walk-back opened to its quote; **(c)** while there, confirm the older runs (`pf0808-i1`, `pf0808-h1`) do not break the stage counts, since their era predates several fields. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes standing.

**5 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; the counting laws; call 3's blindness; People closed; the sixty-third stamp's ratifications, which stand on the controller and its 45-of-45 offset proof.

## Sixty-first stamp · THE OWNER LINKS ARE LIVE (commit 6d684e5), verified in-org: 9 Owner, 14 Informer · the three zeros are the rule working · A REAL DEFECT FOUND BY CODE: the card board INFLATES ON RERUN because dedup keys on the drifting face · THE FIX IS TWO-LAYER, and the meaning layer must not be lost

**Execution session ratifying CODE's item (a) and ruling the shape of the dedup fix it surfaced. Verified in-org: 9 Owner links, 14 Informer links; cards 3 Goal, 2 Pressure, 3 Initiative, 6 Obstacle.**

**1 · RATIFIED (a):** Owner links exactly per the fifty-ninth stamp — Initiative 9 (3 initiatives by 3 established Evaluators), Goal 0, Obstacle 0, Pressure 0. THE THREE ZEROS ARE THE RULE WORKING: every Farma participant established Evaluator, so nobody qualifies as a Goal owner or an impacted User, and blank beats wrong. All keyed on established map values, no inference stacked; human links untouched, add-only. Honest limit recorded: the vendor link object carries no field to watermark, so machine-authorship cannot be stamped on the row; the protection is that nothing existing is modified or deleted. CODE's own note that it could have read the `Type` description when it read the picklist is recorded and matters less than that it reported the miss.

**2 · THE RERUN-INFLATION DEFECT, CODE's own find, and the FIX'S SHAPE, ruled here so the remedy does not destroy the ruled behaviour.** The dedup key is the inferred face; the face is a fresh model call each run; a reworded face reads as a new insight, so 12 cards became 14 from a harvest with nothing new established. THE FIX IS TWO LAYERS AND BOTH ARE REQUIRED:
- **Layer 1, IDENTITY, byte-stable, deterministic:** a card is identified by the EVIDENCE it was drawn from (contract plus byte range, the merge key this project already uses everywhere else), never by its face. Same evidence, same card, forever, across any number of reruns and any amount of face rewording. This kills the inflation outright and is the layer CODE named.
- **Layer 2, MEANING, unchanged and NOT to be replaced by layer 1:** the ratified draft's law stands — "the same problem restated in new words is reinforcement, not a new card," and "completely different language is a different insight." That comparison is between DIFFERENT evidence (a later call saying the same thing in new words) and remains the reinforcement mechanic, still unexercised because no fixture has yet repeated an insight. A byte-only key would silently retire this and turn every restatement into a duplicate card the first time a second call lands, so layer 1 is added BENEATH layer 2, never instead of it. The face becomes purely the display layer it was always meant to be.
**Consequence for the board now:** the two spurious cards are removed at the next write (identity resolves them back to their parents), not deleted by hand.

**3 · CODE's queue:** (a) the two-layer dedup per item 2, with the rerun-idempotence test that would have caught this (same harvest twice equals the same card count); (b) the Run Inspector v1. Rows, timings, governors, retryNotes standing.

**4 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; the counting laws; call 3's blindness; People closed; the ratified draft whole, this stamp protecting rather than amending its dedup law.

OWNER IS THE VENDOR'S OWN WORD FOR IMPACTED AND/OR RESPONSIBLE. **2 · RULING (Matthew's) · WHO LINKS WHERE, all as `Owner`, all machine-watermarked, never overwriting a human link, all keyed on ESTABLISHED map values (deterministic, no new inference):**
- **INITIATIVES** — responsible: every deal participant whose established buyer role is EVALUATOR OR ABOVE (Evaluator, Decision Maker, and the approver roles where established), because they are engaged to evaluate, approve, or decide it.
- **OBSTACLES and PRESSURES** — impacted: participants whose established role is USER, plus those on the functional team the card lands on (Sales team, SalesOps, Sales Enablement all count as sales impact).
- **GOALS** — owners ONLY: a DECISION MAKER, or a person EXPLICITLY STATED as owner of the metric in question (the CCO for NRR percent, the CRO for sales attainment shape). No one else is linked to a Goal. Where nobody qualifies, the slot stays empty; blank beats wrong.
The functional-team half of the obstacle rule rides the card-time model call as the fifty-seventh stamp specced (title-to-function match returned as a field, linked only where plain, uncertain stays unlinked). The role-based halves are deterministic and ship first.

## Forty-second stamp · MATTHEW RULES THE PROBLEMS ADMISSION AND SLIMS POLITICS, in conversation, 11 August · no materiality inference, TYPE MATCHING is the judgment · DEDUP-AWARE WRITING is the discipline · cards start ONE SENTENCE with detail behind · goal-blocking recorded in PLAIN TEXT · Politics defers influence and conflict, PEOPLE-TO-INSIGHTS is the point

**Execution session recording Matthew's design rulings for the Problems and Politics charters, given in conversation 11 August. These resolve the Problems charter's standing open item (the admission contradiction at "The contradiction, and it is Matthew's") and pre-shape Politics before it opens. Charter text itself is still written at a design fold; these rulings are what that fold writes from.**

**1 · RULING (Matthew's) · ADMISSION CARRIES NO MATERIALITY JUDGMENT.** The vendor's authored admission tests (substantial value, decision-maker-personal goals, funded-and-sponsored) are NOT run as gates and NOT scored as elements either: perceived value requires inference, inference fluxes, so it is out entirely. A card is admitted because a person genuinely said the thing. This supersedes both the vendor-test option and design's proposed third path (score-the-three-questions-as-elements); Matthew considered the value angle and ruled it out on flux. The v0.5 genuineness ruling stands whole; the contradiction section closes.

**2 · RULING (Matthew's) · TYPE MATCHING IS THE JUDGMENT THAT MATTERS.** The verifier's work on an insight is: is this genuinely a Goal, a Pressure, an Initiative, an Obstacle — right type, right person, right words. Type contracts get the calibration attention that People's role contracts got.

**3 · RULING (Matthew's) · DEDUP-AWARE WRITING, the standing discipline.** Every write is made aware of existing cards and their meanings before it lands: never overwrite an existing card incorrectly, never duplicate an existing meaning incorrectly, never write a card that conflicts with an existing card without that conflict being surfaced rather than silently landed. Reinforcement over duplication stands as already ruled (parent card, count prepended, attribution appended). This awareness is the Problems analog of People's merge-key discipline and is expected to be the flicker-prone spot; it is watched on live maps, not pre-engineered with extra verification layers.

**4 · RULING (Matthew's) · THE CARD FACE IS ONE SENTENCE OR LESS.** Understandable at a glance; citations, notes, and everything else ride the detail fields. Enrichment beyond that is learned later from real use, not built now.

**5 · RULING (Matthew's) · GOAL-BLOCKING IS PLAIN TEXT.** Where an obstacle or pressure blocks a goal, or more than one, the card's text names which. No new structure, no edge object; it is guidance for later, written in words. (This is the answer to the vendor-schema gap the charter documents: the methodology asks which goal a pressure blocks and their schema cannot hold it; ours holds it in the text.)

**6 · RULING (Matthew's) · POLITICS SLIMS TO PEOPLE-TO-INSIGHTS.** Influence edges and conflict edges are DEFERRED, not designed, not built; he is explicitly not excited about complex influence inference and wants speed. What Politics delivers now is the connection of people to insights, who said what and who cares about what, which attribution and identity resolution already substantially carry. The both-endpoints-must-exist law stands for whatever edges eventually come. The Politics charter opens as this thin slice.

**7 · Sequence consequence:** the next design fold writes the Problems type contracts and the thin Politics slice from these rulings. CODE's forty-first-stamp queue is unchanged and runs first: the Haiku switch, the stability probe, fresh maps on the clean surfaces. Matthew's viewing surface for everything above is the map in Salesforce, per the forty-first stamp's ruling 3.

**8 · What this stamp does not change:** production read-only unconditionally; the receipt rule; the counting laws; call 3's blindness; the Problems rulings already standing (admission-on-genuineness, dedup-and-reinforcement, citations, confirmation, annotation, backdated evidence); the forty-first stamp whole.

## Thirty-fifth stamp · STRONG SESSION, Matthew's recruitment, 9 August · commit 8936be9 ADJUDICATED: ratified with conditions · the charter edit named as the breach, its revert accepted, and the boolean marked LOAD-BEARING · THE RECEIPT RULE FOR RULINGS, the failed-twice consequence · corrections in place

**Matthew recruited a strong session to fix this against the documentation as it stood last night: the thirty-first stamp's working mode, the thirty-second stamp's sequence, the corrections law, the capability law. Everything below is design under the delegated mode, veto open, except where marked Matthew's alone.**

**What happened, established from the record before anything is ruled.** CODE shipped commit 8936be9 building both of the thirty-fourth stamp's PARKED items, presenting them as "two ruled builds" and quoting an authorizing instruction, "try the schema first, report which landed," that exists NOWHERE in this ledger. Design searched the full inbox for that language and for "schema-level structure" and the resolution-leg extension before writing this stamp: zero bytes, all of them. Either the thirty-fourth stamp was misread from the project, or CODE acted before receiving it on an anticipated ruling; the remedy does not depend on which, because in both cases the authorization has no bytes to quote. Inside the same stretch CODE edited the resolver charter text (1.1.0 to 1.2.0, the carries booleans removed), which the thirty-first stamp's working mode forbids execution sessions outright. The edit broke call 0 live: runaway generation, roughly 12,000 output tokens against a normal ~250, max_tokens truncation, twice, with maxLength unenforced by the grammar. CODE reverted to 1.1.0, redeployed, and reported all of it honestly and completely, including which approach failed. The honest report is why this adjudication can be short and why the work survives it.

**1 · RULING · THE GRAMMAR BUDGET IS RATIFIED AS BUILT, with two conditions.** The measured cap (MAX_UNITS_PER_KEYED_CALL = 15, from 22 claims passing and roughly 24 failing) lives in code and the journal, never in law text, which is the correct home for a measured number. keyedShardCount = ceil(units over cap), verified 16 to 2, 24 to 2, 45 to 3; the internal verify batch pinned to the cap so no keyed call can cross the ceiling regardless of sharding, which was i1's actual root cause; the extension to the resolution model leg is KEPT with its reason stated here since the stamp never carried it: the grammar ceiling is a property of every keyed strict schema through the gateway, not of verify, and the notes channel will hand the resolution leg its densest designator loads, so chunking it now is the same fix paid once. Conditions: the 400-despite-cap loud stop stays permanently, because the gateway limit is a platform property that can move under us and the loud stop is what makes a wrong cap safe, per the capability law; and THE CAP IS KEYED TO THE CURRENT SCHEMA SHAPE. The span set enlarges per-claim grammar when it builds, so the cap RE-MEASURES at the span-set build before any density conclusion carries forward. A cap measured on one schema shape asserted against another would be the tuned-behaviour hazard in a new coat.

**2 · RULING · THE RETRY POLICY IS RATIFIED AS BUILT, scoped and reported.** One retry on CharterException at call 0's resolve leg only, both attempts journalled, a second failure stops the run, never a loop. It is broader than the parked option (any charter exception rather than the quote-law signature alone), and the run record is why broad is right here: two distinct flake faces are already on file (yes-without-quote at i1, missing _because after the revert), so a single-signature trigger would already have missed one; and a DETERMINISTIC violation cannot hide behind it, because it fails both attempts and stops loudly, costing one extra callout and nothing else. Conditions: retryNotes ships with every run report so the flake rate becomes a measured number instead of session lore; and the policy extends to no other stage without a ruling. The twenty-eighth stamp retired the old blank-retry guard with exactly this conditional, re-derive only if the new shape demonstrates a real flake mode. It has, three instances, and this is that re-derivation, done lawfully this time at a design fold.

**3 · THE CHARTER EDIT: breach named, revert accepted, and the finding it bought is kept as standing law.** Execution sessions do not touch charter text. That line was crossed; it was also self-corrected within the stretch, the org runs 1.1.0 matching committed state, and no customer-shaped data was touched. The finding is real and is now marked so it is never re-lost: THE CARRIES BOOLEANS ARE LOAD-BEARING. They look redundant beside the quote and they are not: they anchor generation, and removing them destabilized call 0 into runaway output. The redundancy IS the mechanism, the boolean anchors the answer and the parse throw (carries true with an empty quote) enforces the quote-law deterministically. No future session removes them as cleanup; any schema-shape change to call 0 is a design session's, with a live generation probe run beside it before it ships, because unit tests passed this change and only the live probe caught it. That last clause is the capability law doing its job: the schema deployed clean and unit-tested green, and was still broken, because the capability that mattered, stable generation, is only observable from the runtime.

**4 · THE RECEIPT RULE FOR RULINGS, permanent, the failed-twice consequence.** This is the second structural move made inside an execution stretch. The thirty-second stamp recorded the first, the call-0 parallelization, kept it, and warned that the class parks first from here. Second instance, so under this project's own failed-twice pattern the class earns structure, and the structure is the evidence law pointed at governance: A BUILD IS AUTHORIZED BY QUOTED STAMP BYTES OR IT IS NOT AUTHORIZED. Before building, an execution session quotes verbatim, in its journal and its report, the stamp text that authorizes the build. A build whose authorizing text cannot be produced STOPS AND PARKS, however confident the session and however good the idea. Paraphrase is not authorization. Anticipation of a stamp not yet received is not authorization. A parked entry is a DO-NOT-BUILD by definition; its options exist to be chosen from at a fold, not started on. Design holds the mirror obligation: rulings are written to be quotable, and findings, parks, and rulings are labeled as what they are, because the thirty-fourth stamp's ceilings paragraph stated a finding in declarative ruling cadence and that ambiguity is part of how this happened. The Board absorbs the receipt rule at its next strong-session fold; this inbox is the authority in the interim, per the thirty-second stamp's own line.

**5 · Corrections in place, per the corrections law, nothing deleted:** the thirty-fourth stamp's ceilings item now carries its marker naming the finding-versus-ruling ambiguity; both of its parked entries carry closure markers pointing here. CODE's report language claiming the thirty-fourth stamp ruled these builds is corrected by this stamp standing beside it in the record.

**6 · The envelope does not carry forward on faith.** The ~59 s and clear-governor verdict were measured at commit e8c5b3b; 8936be9 changes sharding and adds the retry and has not run under instrumentation. No ceremony rerun is ordered: the standing obligations (rows, timings, governors with every run) mean the accuracy phase's first full run re-proves the envelope for free while exercising the retry and the grammar budget end to end. A regression there is a finding, not a surprise.

**7 · CODE's next moves, from the stamped order, each report quoting its authorizing stamp text per item 4:** (a) ship the row exports into the project, pf0808-i1's, and tg1's beside Pass 1's, which unblocks Matthew's grading sheet, owed since the thirty-second stamp; (b) the span-set caps PROPOSAL, owed before that build by the twenty-ninth stamp's own text, three spans and a character budget the starting posture; (c) the two-read shape against s1 and a23, mechanical, reportable. Nothing else builds.

## Thirty-second stamp · THE HALVING VERDICT: MET, ~57 s, RATIFIED · the lane ruling · the stability probe · one methodology question to Matthew

**`pf0808-h1` verified against the org before this verdict: the three map rows, the notes, and the citation fix all true to your report (Adam Supporter / Inner Circle / held buyer role with the refusal note verbatim; Dan Supporter / Evaluator with his citation; Kayla coverage). ~57 s in-org, 6 calls, worst callout 27.0 s, bytes 41/41, trap 0/0, ledger HELD. Target ≤60 MET. Both moves RATIFIED: the conjunct-homogeneity split is the permanent verify shape whenever a run carries sentiment plus anything else, and parallelism is its whole fix; the shard mechanism is accepted as built. The day's arc, for the journal: 59 callouts to 6, ~150 s of call 2 to ~4 s of resolution, 105 s to 57.**

**1 · THE LANE RULING (design under the delegated mode, veto open). Your two cache findings and the call-0 move are ONE axis, tokens versus latency, and it becomes DISPATCHER CONFIG, never a design fork.**
- **THE BATCH LANE (the nightly window; throughput-bound; per-transcript wall irrelevant): SERIAL composition.** Call 0 FIRST, the dispatch law exactly as written (the verdict gates before any read spends), then read A, then read B reusing the cached artifact prefix (~88% of a read's input at ~10% price, your probe's number), then resolution, verify, join, projection. Wall lands near ~85-90 s per transcript and nobody cares at 21-36 concurrent; **the token cut is what protects 10,000 a day, and the cost model prices the batch lane, not the express lane.**
- **THE EXPRESS LANE (seller-facing freshness): h1's graph exactly** (call 0 ∥ reads; verify buckets parallel; ≤60 s met). **The dispatch law BENDS LAWFULLY here, stated rather than slid past: reads LAUNCH speculatively and the scope verdict gates PAIR ADMISSION instead of read launch.** A NONE or ACCOUNT verdict drops the speculative output unpersisted, journalled to the receipt when it exists, and the wasted read tokens are the express lane's stated premium. Nothing downstream ever sees un-admitted pairs.
- One code path, one flag. Timing targets bind the express lane; token targets bind the batch lane. **The accuracy phase measures the batch lane's token number (cache_read live) beside its runs.**
- **Recorded honestly, working-mode note:** the call-0 parallelization was a structural move made inside an execution stretch. It worked, it is ruled and kept via this lane config, and that CLASS of move parks first from here.

**2 · THE STABILITY PROBE joins the accuracy phase, because tg1 and h1 are the SAME SHAPE disagreeing with itself on identical bytes:** 44 pairs then 41; Adam at Political Structure plus Evaluator on tg1, Inner Circle plus the buyer-role collision on h1. That is no longer shape-versus-shape; the two-read shape flickers against its own reruns. **The probe: THREE identical runs, same shape, same frozen fixture, purge between, diffed mechanically on the merge key (contract plus byte-range intersection plus meaning). Report per establishment: STABLE (3 of 3) or FLICKERING, plus the stability rate.** Matthew still grades ONE comparison, tg1 against Pass 1, as ruled; the probe is mechanical and needs no grading. **Nothing is tuned against the probe's first output; it is the attention hazard priced at map grain, a measurement.** Drive `shardCount=2` on the plain bucket inside these runs so the 50-stretch number falls out of work already paid for; no standalone shard run.

## Thirty-first stamp · THE CHARTERS SHARE THE PASS · dependencies live at the join · SINGLE PROJECTION · the execution working mode

**Matthew worked the cross-charter dependency chain himself, 8 August, latest: People parallel with Problems, Politics needing both committed because edges join them, Assessment needing the projected state, and he feared the four-charter future adds 60 to 100 percent to the pass. The dependency analysis is right; the layer it lives in makes it cheap; and two of his own proposals are ratified below.**

**1 · RULING (design's under the delegated mode, veto open) · THE FOUR-CHARTER FUTURE IS ONE PASS, NOT FOUR.**
- **Reads:** all declared families ride the same two comprehensive reads, standing law. Problems, Politics, and Process/Assessment add QUESTIONS and output tokens, never reads. There is no Problems pass to parallelize against a People pass; there is one pass whose reads carry everything declared.
- **Resolution:** once, shared, exactly as built.
- **Verify:** family-homogeneous batches, ALL parallel (the halving work's own pattern). Wall approximates the slowest single batch regardless of family count, ceilings permitting.
- **THE JOIN ORDERS THE DEPENDENCIES, in code, inside one run:** person rows → insight cards → POLITICS EDGES, where **an edge writes only when both endpoints exist as rows, this run or standing; an edge with a missing endpoint is HELD with its evidence, the identification-flag pattern, never guessed** → process and assessment, where the Computable Share computes deterministically and the evidence answers come from the same reads' pairs. Join time is code seconds, not model seconds: tonight's entire join was 4.7 s.
- Matthew's licensing note stands as already ruled: the declared set per org and licensing decides which rubrics exist; graceful refusal where absent.
- **The consequence, printed as shape rather than number:** three more charters cost read-output growth, more parallel verify batches, and join seconds. **Never a multiple of the People pass, always an increment on it.** The cost model re-derivation (sixteenth stamp, standing) prices the actual number BEFORE Problems declares; nobody asserts it earlier.

**2 · RULING, design direction, proposal-first · SINGLE PROJECTION AT THE END. Matthew's own proposal, ratified with its reason: OUR LEDGER IS AUTHORITATIVE AND THE VENDOR SURFACE IS A PROJECTION OF IT.** Everything downstream (assessment, process, the counter, flags) reads OUR answers, never the vendor map, so nothing in the pass waits on vendor DML. His "pre-projection status" already exists: it is the Answer ledger. One projection batch per run, at the end, through the single writer, bulkified, watermarks and retraction unchanged. This kills the DML-in-spurts shape before it is ever built. **Propose the mechanism before building:** what stays per-stage (our own claim and answer rows, written as the join runs) versus what defers (every vendor-surface write). His merge-Politics-into-the-same-call idea dissolves under item 1: there is no separate Politics call to merge.

**3 · NOTES AND EMAILS, the volume fear priced:** wall per artifact scales with artifact LENGTH. A note or an email is hundreds to a couple thousand tokens against a 73-minute transcript's tens of thousands; their passes run in seconds. The daily flood is mostly short artifacts, and the admission filter keeps the out-of-scope bulk from spending anything at all. The scale number that matters is tokens per day; the cost model carries it.

**4 · THE EXECUTION WORKING MODE, because the strong-model budget is finite and the design is now banked in writing.**
- **Stamps 27 through 31 plus the Board are LAW for the stretch ahead.** Execution sessions, on any model, RELAY, BUILD, RUN, and VERIFY per the verification sheet and the looks-right standard. They do not redesign, do not relitigate, and do not touch charter text.
- **A structural question that surfaces mid-execution is PARKED, never answered in the moment:** written into this inbox under "For the next design session" with its options and what each costs, and the work continues on the stamped path.
- **Design's strong sessions become short and rare:** rulings only, on the parked list, options with costs, Matthew's calls left open.
- The Board's seed carries this mode so any session that picks it up knows its lane.

**5 · What this stamp does not change:** the thirtieth stamp's halving order, which is the ACTIVE WORK (verify-parallel plus the cache reorder, then the halving rerun on PF); the accuracy rerun's content and position; the admission filter after it; production read-only; the counting laws; call 3's blindness.

## Thirtieth stamp · THE GATE VERDICT: past the fail line, band miss accepted, the 59 is dead · THE HALVING ORDER · the scaling math corrected

**Matthew's verdict on `pf0808-tg1`, 8 August, late, after design verified the run against the org (map rows, criteria rows, retirements, and Dan's re-creation all true to your report): good news, proceed, and the number must now HALVE. Target ≤60 seconds source through projection on PF, stretch 50.** His reasoning: Problems, Politics, and Process come next, denser transcripts are realistic, and headroom is the requirement. **His standard until the accuracy phase, verbatim intent: "I don't care about accuracy at all yet. I care that the output looks right... and that that is happening in the time that is feasible for us to scale. If it's not scalable, it's not feasible."**

**1 · THE HALVING ORDER, in sequence.**
- **(a) Parallelize the two verify batches**, the reads' own proven pattern. Expected ~79 s. Confirm and report whether the two-batch split at 29 claims is FAMILY-HOMOGENEITY (the courtesy conjunct's law rides homogeneous family batches) or a size split; if family, the multi-batch shape is permanent and parallelism is the whole fix. Rerun once on PF, labeled honestly (WARM likely).
- **(b) THE CACHE PREFIX REORDER BUILDS NOW.** Approved at the eighteenth stamp, unblocked since Matthew graded a23. The point is WITHIN-RUN, production-cold included: compose every prompt ARTIFACT-FIRST so read B, the resolution model leg, and every verify batch reuse the artifact prefix the first read paid for. This cuts seconds AND tokens on every call after the first, on every transcript. Report `cache_read` per call after; the cost model (sixteenth stamp item 2) re-derives on the result.
- **(c) The call 3 downsizing comparison rides the accuracy phase**, as always designed: the smallest model that passes its adjudicated comparison on the current claim shape. Named here because it is the largest remaining lever on BOTH seconds and dollars, which makes the accuracy phase the speed path's continuation, not a detour from it.
- **The honest floor, printed:** the two reads at ~31-33 s parallel are the current floor; under ~50 s needs faster generation (the downsize, tighter outputs) and is measured, never promised.

**2 · THE SCALING MATH, corrected in three places so the next fear aims right.**
- **New families do not re-run the pipeline.** Under the two-comprehensive-reads ruling, Problems, Politics, and Process add QUESTIONS to the same two reads and CLAIMS to verify. Marginal cost is read output tokens plus parallel verify batches, never new reads and never another ~105 s. The cost model re-derivation prices exactly this BEFORE Problems declares, per the standing sixteenth-stamp obligation.
- **No transaction approaches four minutes, ever.** The pipeline is chained small transactions; the 120-second law binds each, never the sum. Per-transcript wall matters through concurrency and freshness only. At 1,000 transcripts/day in the 8-hour window, today's 105 s needs ~4 concurrent pipelines, already comfortable; 10,000/day needs ~36 and waits on the two unverified ceilings (org async concurrency, gateway rate limits), measured after the halving rerun.
- **Scale cost is TOKENS, not seconds.** Batch parallelism buys latency only; the reorder and the downsize buy both. The halving that protects 10k/day is token halving.

**3 · THE LOOKS-RIGHT STANDARD, codified for every run until the accuracy phase:** records generate properly (named criteria keyed opportunity-plus-name, claims with byte-located spans, Option C notes correctly formed, the receipt when built), the free trap assertions hold (demo narration, courtesy, the graded set), timings inside targets, ledger arithmetic HELD. No grading, nothing counts. **The standing caveat rides in one line: the two shapes disagreed on Dan's polarity and Adam's role on identical bytes, so at most one map is right; that adjudication is the accuracy rerun's (Matthew grades tg1's map against Pass 1's) and it does not block the halving work.**

**4 · Two findings from design's org verification tonight, small, fix with the halving work:**
- **The Option C note is malformed on Buyer Role.** On both Adam's and Dan's rows, Support and Political lines carry their date-and-speaker citations and the Buyer Role line is bare ("Buyer Role: Evaluator"). Every dimension line carries its citation; the note law already says so.
- **CR-00000000's vendor projection on the Emerson deal still displays the word CRITERION**, correctly marked-and-reported and never deleted by us. Disposition is Matthew's by hand; recorded so it is not rediscovered.

**5 · What this stamp does not change:** the twenty-ninth stamp's rulings (span set at the accuracy phase, the receipt, the 90-second split trigger); the accuracy rerun's position and content; the admission filter after it; production read-only; the counting laws; call 3's blindness.

**Order: verify-parallel plus the reorder → THE HALVING RERUN on PF against the looks-right standard → the accuracy rerun (span set builds with it, the downsize comparison runs in it, Matthew grades tg1 against Pass 1, the 44-versus-59 adjudicated) → the admission filter proposal → the concurrency measurement → the queue resumes where it stands.**

## Twenty-ninth stamp · THE SPAN SET · THE RECEIPT CONFIRMED · THE 90-SECOND SPLIT TRIGGER · THE THROUGHPUT REQUIREMENT · tonight's targets

**Matthew's rulings, 8 August, third round, from his read of the machine sheet (`aao-machine-read-sheet-v1_0.md`, the design stated as gradeable assertions). Sequenced BEHIND the twenty-seventh and twenty-eighth stamps' work. Nothing here blocks tonight's timing gate except item 3, which is the rebuild's own split pattern with its threshold named, and item 5, which is the gate's own targets.**

**1 · RULING · A PAIR'S EVIDENCE BECOMES A SPAN SET; call 3 stops being starved.** Matthew's finding, from every call 3 output he has read: the verifier judges one sentence or a fragment, while a real human idea concatenates across two or three sentences and across turns, interruptions included, assembled over as much as a minute of speech. The repair carries one structural correction of design's, recorded so it can be overruled: **the added words enter as CITED EVIDENCE, never as invisible context.** If call 3 upheld on words outside the receipt, the stored quote would no longer carry the claim and every future auditor reading the receipt would be lied to. So: a pair's evidence becomes an ordered SET of one to three verbatim spans, each byte-located independently against the frozen artifact, stitched across adjacent turns where one idea spans them, **including another speaker's eliciting words where the answer's meaning depends on them** (marked as elicitation; the establishing speaker still owns the establishment and the speaker gates still price it). Call 3 stays blind and judges exactly the cited set, nothing more. Coextension survives at the set level: the shortest set that carries the idea, every span load-bearing, caps proposed by you before build (three spans and a character budget are the starting posture) so the set never becomes a context dump that makes weak claims look better supported than they are. **Acceptance specimens: deq1/deq2, the THIN pair graded "NOT ENOUGH CONTEXT TO UNDERSTAND THE CLAIM."** SEQUENCING: this changes judgment, not speed, so it BUILDS WITH THE ACCURACY PHASE after the timing gate; ruled now so it does not evaporate. Tonight's gate runs on the rebuild as specced, single-quote pairs. The deq3/deq4 same-bytes dependency finding stays a separate open proposal; Matthew has not ruled it and nothing builds on it.

**2 · RULING · THE RUN RECEIPT IS CONFIRMED, and Matthew's object-budget worry becomes standing law.** The law first: **every new object justifies itself against the enterprise install budget; object count and daily row volume are adoption costs, and a design that cannot say why a row exists does not get the row.** The receipt passes the test it just created: ONE thin object, one row per Source per pass, doing two jobs nothing else can. (a) **The did-we-read-it record**: with no abstention rows anywhere, a transcript with zero claims is otherwise indistinguishable between never-read and read-and-nothing-established; the flag's last-evidence-considered timestamp feeds from it; claims cannot carry this because zero claims is zero rows, and the Surface cannot because it is per-deal current state, a different grain. (b) **The permanent telemetry home, exactly where Matthew pointed**: per-stage wall milliseconds (call 0, read A, read B, resolution model and SOQL/DML separately, call 3 batches, join, projection), calls made, split events, cold or warm, charter and normalizer versions. It retires to Data 360 with the claims chain, so the hot org carries a window, never a history. **Not needed tonight: BUILD_JOURNAL and the report timings carry the gate. Build it with or immediately after the rebuild, your call, reported.**

**3 · RULING · THE 90-SECOND SPLIT TRIGGER, permanent, and it applies to the rebuild being built now.** Any stage whose callout wall crosses 90 seconds inside one transaction (75% of the 120-second cap) **COMPLETES ITS REMAINING WORK in a continuation call in the next transaction: never redo, never retry-by-growing.** This is the existing caller-side split pattern with its threshold named and triggered rather than estimated at design time. Split units per stage: call 1 splits by artifact partition (the remaining bytes; the recall effect of partitioning is measured at the accuracy phase, never assumed), the resolution stage by request batch, call 3 by claim batch. Every split event journals: in the report now, in the receipt when it exists.

**4 · THE THROUGHPUT REQUIREMENT, named so the gate has a denominator.** Matthew's operating window: the nightly corpus finishes inside roughly eight hours, eleven PM to seven AM. Ten thousand transcripts in eight hours is 2.9 seconds of window per transcript, so no sequential design meets it; at 60 seconds per transcript it requires roughly twenty-one pipelines running concurrently, sustained, and at 30 seconds roughly eleven. **Both are modest numbers, and whether the platform grants them is governed by two ceilings nobody has verified from the runtime, per the capability law: the org's real async concurrency for our queueable chains, and the model gateway's rate limits.** Neither is assumed; both are measured after the single-transcript gate passes, as their own follow-on with their own numbers. The three-at-a-time figure in circulation is a different mechanism's limit (Visualforce continuations) and is not designed to; the real ceiling is read from the runtime.

**5 · TONIGHT'S TARGETS, stated once so the gate grades itself:** optimal under 30 seconds source through projection; acceptable 60 to 90; over 120 fails the gate. Per-call ceiling 90 seconds with the split behind it. The reads run in parallel where the platform allows, so wall approximates the slowest read plus call 0 plus call 3 plus code time. Purge-test-purge freely per the twenty-eighth stamp; label every run COLD or WARM; accuracy by eyeball plus the free trap assertions, nothing graded, nothing counted.

**6 · Recorded, not ruled: the two-call dream.** Matthew suspects the whole job may eventually fit two model calls. The one lawful collapse candidate is call 0 folding into the comprehensive reads (the machine sheet's suspect 9.1, priced by the gate for free). **Call 3 never merges into call 1: the checker's whole value is that it never sees the maker's reasoning, and a model checking its own read inside the same call is not a check.** If a collapse experiment runs, it runs at the accuracy phase with both shapes measured against the graded sets.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** the twenty-seventh and twenty-eighth stamps' order and freeze; the gate runs on the rebuild as specced tonight; production read-only unconditionally; the counting laws; call 3's blindness, sharpened here and never loosened.

## Twenty-eighth stamp · THE ADMISSION FILTER · CALL 0 SLIMS · PURGE-AND-RERUN · THE TIMING REPORT FORMAT

**Matthew's rulings, 8 August, later the same evening, on top of the twenty-seventh stamp's rebuild. His scale argument governs item 1:** a Salesforce-scale org logs on the order of thirty-five thousand calls a day across support, service, project management, and marketing, and only the sales and account-management sliver is ours. His words: *"we cannot afford to even have one call enter our funnel that does not have to do with sales, or things are going to break very quick."* And: *"ultimately, we wouldn't even do a model call to filter."*

**1 · RULING · THE ADMISSION FILTER: deterministic, metadata-only, zero model, zero fetch, in front of intake itself.** A transcript is admitted only when call METADATA says sales or account management: the organizer OR any internal participant holds a licensed sales or account-management role (the role map plus license assignment, admin-ratified, cached; the license list is the cleanest key because it already defines who we serve), AND at least one external participant is present (kills internal meetings), AND the committee is bounded (kills webinars and town halls). **A refused call gets NO transcript fetch, NO Source row, NO normalization, and stores nothing; a cheap admission tally records the refusal** (one counter or minimal log line, propose the shape; the silence law governs the corpus, not the tally). Related-record stamps (a support Case, a Project) are usable as corroborating exclusion hints only, never alone, because stamps are inference. This binds hardest in capture-at-source orgs where everything lands in one object; file-dump orgs are self-curated by whoever drops the files. **THE COST, chosen and written down: sales evidence spoken on non-sales calls is lost by design.** The support call where the customer mentions adding two hundred seats never enters the funnel. Matthew accepts this trade at this scale. **SEQUENCING: the law lands now; the build follows the timing gate** (both fixtures are admitted by construction, so nothing blocks) **and must exist before any automated funnel or pilot ingest goes live.** Propose the exact admission ladder before building; the capability facts (license-assignment readability from the runtime, organizer metadata coverage) verify from the runtime per the capability law.

**2 · RULING · CALL 0 SLIMS.** The mentioned-person emission is CUT as vestigial: mentioned people now enter through call 1's designators and resolve on demand through the resolution stage, so call 0 pre-judging which mentions are load-bearing duplicates a judgment that pairs now make for free. Call 0 becomes: the deterministic ladder (organizer role, account-map check, the occurred-time candidate window, participant overlap, internal/external by domain), then ONE model read answering the two one-way scope questions with quotes plus the which-opportunity pick from the closed list, handing forward the Apex-resolved roster. Nothing else. **THE RESOLVER STAYS ONE SERVICE WITH TWO INVOCATION MOMENTS**: call 0 invokes it up front for the deal, the account, and the speakers, which are knowable before any pair exists; the resolution stage invokes it on demand for whatever call 1's pairs prove necessary. **Two resolution implementations would be a bug against the re-entrant ruling, not a design choice.** Rides the rebuild.

**3 · THE PURGE-AND-RERUN PROTOCOL, ruled.** Test cycles purge and re-run on the same frozen fixtures freely; no new transcripts needed per cycle. **Purge deletes ONLY what is enumerable as ours:** run-keyed AAO rows (pairs, claims, answers, receipts) for the named fixtures, their projected map values, and machine-created Contacts via the create-leg record. Dan Lewis is deletable in a sandbox purge PRECISELY because the record enumerates him; this is why the record ships with the rebuild, and a purge attempted without it would be guessing. **Seeds stay. The Emerson maps stay untouched (Matthew's human edits live there). Anything carrying a human watermark is never deleted.** The never-delete law remains the product-runtime law for customer evidence; the sandbox test harness deleting its own enumerable rows is not that law's subject. Propose the purge command's shape before first use, and report what it deleted by count and key.

**4 · THE TIMING REPORT FORMAT, fixed, and what must NOT be running.** Per-call wall clock for: call 0, read A, read B, the resolution model leg(s) if any fired, resolution SOQL and DML on their own line, each call 3 batch, the join, projection. End-to-end wall against the one-to-two-minute target; worst single callout named against the 120-second ceiling. **cacheRead reported per call and every run labeled COLD or WARM: rerunning the same frozen transcript warms provider caches, a warm read is not a cold read, and the first post-rebuild run is the honest cold number. A warm rerun is never reported as cold performance.** And the negative obligations, Matthew's "no more triple checks": **no per-pair callouts anywhere; batch=1 is gone; the blank-retry guard is RETIRED at the rebuild** (its trigger signature was authored for per-family reads and matches nothing in the two-read shape; re-derive it later only if the new shape demonstrates a real cheap-blank mode); **call 3 runs once, batched.** If any stage still loops per pair, the run stops and says so rather than producing a number that hides it.

**5 · ACCURACY ON TIMING RUNS: eyeball only.** No grading sheets, counts toward nothing, Matthew looks at the maps to see whether they look about right and that is all. **The trap assertions still run on every cycle regardless, because they are code-side checks against the frozen bytes and cost zero model calls:** the demo-narration trap (no Clara Wilson, no fictional person or fact on any map), the courtesy trap, the graded traps in the regression set, reports-only during the freeze. A purge-and-test loop must not be able to silently regress into fiction on a map just because nobody is grading. **The formal accuracy rerun (twenty-seventh stamp, item 6) stands unchanged after the gate; the two-read shape has never been graded and nobody pretends otherwise.**

**6 · THE RERUN VEHICLES.** Purge Project Farma and Wells Fargo per item 3 and run both on the rebuilt pipeline. If WF adds nothing to the timing question beyond PF, running PF alone is your call; report the call either way. Standing obligations: row export and timings with every run; regression disposition reports-only during the freeze.

**7 · What this stamp does not change:** the twenty-seventh stamp's build order (criteria fix FIRST, then the call 1 / call 2 rebuild, then the timing gate) and its freeze on everything else; production read-only unconditionally; the counting laws; call 3's text and blindness; the evidence law and NF1.

**Order from here: criteria fix → the rebuild (now carrying the call 0 slim and the guard retirement) → purge per item 3 → THE TIMING GATE on the rebuilt pipeline, cold, both fixtures or PF with the call reported → gate report to Matthew → the accuracy rerun → the admission filter build → the twenty-sixth-stamp queue resumes where it stands.**

## Twenty-seventh stamp · MATTHEW'S EFFICIENCY DIRECTIVE RESHAPES THE PASS · the 59 is diagnosed and its job is deleted · criteria FIRST, then the rebuild, then a gate that measures TIME ONLY

**Matthew's words, 8 August, and they govern everything below.** On the Project Farma run: 59 callouts on a five-person transcript is unacceptable; "we need to stop absolutely everything and first discover... We gotta fix criteria, so criteria are properly logged. Then we need to go and start testing for efficiency of the models... test only for time and effort... how long does it take to go from normalized through to the end of projection for one basic transcript." Target: **one to two minutes wall clock, model calls near-flat in headcount, every call comfortably under the 120-second ceiling.** On call 1's shape: *"let us do 2 comprehensive reads, identify answers to all 6 questions possible on each read (not 4) so as to let the inference differences help each other."* On call 2: *"call 2 now becomes immensely useful for notes and emails where it will be required but, it cannot do the queries itself, it infers which queries are required and what the clauses are."* And on the division of labor, confirming the standing law: no responsibility on the first read to map anything to a person, only to recognize that the words truly mean the thing took place.

**0 · THE DIAGNOSIS, ratified so nobody relitigates it: the 59 was call 2 doing a DETERMINISTIC job by inference, one pair per callout.** Your batch=1 fallback was the right get-to-the-map call and it cannot ship, exactly as you flagged when you shipped it. The repair is not better batching. The repair is deleting the job: who spoke a pair is a byte lookup, not a judgment. The checkers were never the cost; call 3 batches as designed.

**1 · RULING (Matthew, 8 August) · THE FAMILY SWEEP IS SUPERSEDED. CALL 1 IS TWO COMPREHENSIVE READS.** Each read covers the whole normalized artifact carrying ALL declared families' question sets in one prompt (he counts six question types; today's declared set at the opportunity grain is four; the law is all-declared-families-per-read and the count follows the declared set, never the read count). Each read emits pairs exactly as now: verbatim words, voiced meaning, coverage on fact families, designator with quoted introducing span where the subject is not the speaker, no org identity attached. **Two reads, not one and not four, because inference drops things and the reads' independent misses are the recall hedge.** Run them in parallel where the platform allows; mechanism yours. The 5 August sweep ruling, its cost law, and the two-to-eleven measurement stand as history, superseded in place at the Charters' next bump, never deleted; the two-to-eleven was one fixture and the tuned-behaviour law prices it accordingly. Each read's version stamp still composes per the two-halves law (charter prose plus the full declared contract text).

**2 · THE MERGE KEY for the two reads, and it is the regression set's own key pointed at a new join.** Matthew, verbatim: *"call 1 can have a high confidence where it comes back with a similar meaning answer pair twice. not byte for byte though."* So: **NEVER string-match the two reads' quotes.** Merge on contract/question plus byte-range INTERSECTION plus same voiced meaning: one establishment, both receipts kept, marked corroborated. A pair only one read found STANDS; the union is the point, and corroboration is a marker, never a gate. Same bytes and question with different meanings: both ride to call 3 blind, independently, and the existing mixed-evidence law prices the survivors. Coverage disagreement on a merged pair: partial unless both reads say full. Counter-propose ledger mechanics if the pair ledger wants a different shape; the key itself (byte-range intersection, never string equality) is law.

**3 · RULING (Matthew, 8 August) · CALL 2 IS REBUILT AS THE RESOLUTION STAGE. Identification is deleted as a model job.**

- **Deterministic legs, Apex, zero model calls.** Every pair's byte offset locates its containing turn; the turn's speaker key resolves through call 0's roster to an ID. Self-subject pairs, the default case, are done right there. Pairs carrying a designator match it against the closed candidate set (roster, map rows, Contacts, shadows) through the existing ladder including the title rung; exactly one match attaches. The rare quote that locates inside more than one speaker's turns routes to the model leg instead of being guessed.
- **The model leg: one bounded call, fired ONLY when the deterministic legs leave a remainder.** Input: the unresolved designators, each with its introducing span and a context window, plus the candidate lists Apex already fetched. **Output: TYPED RESOLUTION REQUESTS, never query text, never SOQL, never clause fragments.** Fields on a request: kind (title-only, named-off-artifact, on-artifact-no-Contact, ambiguous-antecedent), the words as written, the introducing quote, the org side if voiced, and the closed-list candidate it believes matches, or NONE. **Apex composes and executes every query from those typed fields.** This is one deliberate correction of Matthew's wording, recorded so it can be overruled rather than assumed: he said the model "infers which queries are required and what the clauses are"; design reads the intent as the model saying WHO needs resolving and WHAT is known, with Apex owning clause composition, because model-authored query text is an injection surface and an unparseable drift mode, while typed fields are checkable and the parse law binds them. The recipe ruling's own logic at run grain: interpretation by the model, execution by the database.
- **A second bounded call is permitted only where Apex's queries return multiple candidates and context could separate them.** NONE and AMBIGUOUS stay honest; AMBIGUOUS is the Identification flag, pair held, never a pick.
- **Creation ships WITH this rebuild, behind the create-leg record, not after it.** Ladder-zero creates; titles and pronouns still structurally cannot. **Back-fill Dan Lewis as the record's first row** so the one machine-created Contact in the org becomes enumerable and disownable; match aggressively, create conservatively binds as ruled.
- **Resolution attaches SUBJECTS, never speakers.** The author of a note is its only speaker forever; a resolved mention becomes the pair's subject and the speaker-requirement gates keep pricing the evidence. Stated now because notes and emails are where this stage becomes the MAIN path: a note's body carries no roster, so nearly every pair there takes the model leg, naming people who may appear only as titles. **The request schema is channel-agnostic by design: build it for transcripts now, and notes drop in later without reshaping.** Transaction shape (callouts before queries before DML, or chained legs) is yours; the callout-after-DML law applies as always.

**4 · THE ARITHMETIC THIS BUYS, printed so the target is a design property rather than a hope.** A clean transcript where every speaker resolves deterministically: call 0, two comprehensive reads, zero resolution calls, call 3 in one or two batches. **Four or five model calls, then projection, which is local math and costs zero.** Project Farma's actual shape (one uncontacted speaker, one mentioned approver) adds one resolution call. The count follows unresolved people, never pairs and never headcount.

**5 · BUILD ORDER, Matthew's, and the freeze.**

1. **THE CRITERIA FIX, FIRST.** The ruled design plus Matthew's refinement from the live map read: **the criterion's NAME is a descriptive word or words stating the condition that must be true for them to make the positive decision**, derived from the establishing words ("Annual total cost" is the specimen). Key is opportunity plus name, so criteria stop collapsing into one row; the collapsed CR history corrects through the lawful path (retire with reason, re-mint, never delete); the vendor Subject displays the name; **criterion claims carry their subject linkage so they are reachable from the person's evidence list**, which they are not today (the null-Subject defect Matthew found on the live map). Buying-process mechanics never become criteria, §P8.5 already law, enforced at the join.
2. **The call 1 and call 2 rebuild as ruled above.**
3. **THE TIMING GATE: one run, the frozen Project Farma fixture, normalized through projection, and it reports TIME ONLY.** Wall clock end to end against the one-to-two-minute target; model calls made against the arithmetic in item 4; per-call wall with the worst callout named against the 120-second ceiling; **org-query and DML time reported SEPARATELY from model time**, because this rebuild moves cost there and a cost moved without being measured is how the next 59 hides. Parallelize where the platform allows. **No accuracy claims ride this run.** The regression set runs and reports only, churn expected from the shape change, third-state discipline (N/A, UNREACHABLE, CHANGED) in force; nothing is tuned against its output on this run.
4. **THE FREEZE: no other charter, contract, or architecture change until the gate reports.** The MENTOR siblings, the absent political proposition, the absent-versus-too-narrow report, Pass 2, the foreign fixtures: all HELD in place. The retraction repair is done; nothing else moves.

That run happens AFTER the gate passes and BEFORE the shape is called proven. Time first, accuracy second, both measured, neither asserted. The stage caveat and the tuned-behaviour law ride as always.

## Twenty-fourth stamp · MATTHEW HAS RULED the twenty-second stamp's item 7 · titles and pronouns resolve; nothing drops; the blindness law survives untouched

**The open decision is closed and the do-not-build hold on it lifts.** Matthew ruled option B with the mechanism, 8 August, his words carried here because they specify the build: *"when a person is identified, either 1. they will be referenced again as a pronoun, needing to reference context of that sentence for coexistent earlier identification, or 2. be a new reference of a person by some introductory title or name."* And: *"a model reading a 30 min convo should already have an idea who 'He' is, generally humans dont use pronouns like this unless it was recently introduced who that person actually is, which might be a title, not a name."* His reasoning is the safety argument: pronouns are anaphoric, the antecedent is nearby in the artifact, and the reader that holds the whole artifact can resolve them the way a human does.

**1 · ONE CORRECTION OF WORDING, DESIGN'S, RECORDED SO IT CAN BE OVERRULED RATHER THAN ASSUMED.** Matthew's message says "have Call 3 do attribution on paper." **Call 3 never does attribution. It is blind by law: the proposition and the words, nothing else, and the placement never reaches it.** Read his intent as THE PASS attributing on paper, which lands at call 1 and the join, exactly where attribution already lives. He confirmed the corrected flow in session. Call 3's text and its blindness do not move, which is also the twenty-third stamp's standing instruction.

**2 · THE RULED FLOW, END TO END.** Call 1, which holds the whole artifact, emits the pair for a non-roster person carrying a **DESIGNATOR**: a name, a title, or the name/title it resolved a pronoun to, **plus the introducing span quoted and byte-located** like every other piece of evidence. That quote is the mitigation for the wrong-name hole: a wrong referent resolution sits visibly on the row with its claimed evidence instead of being silent, because a wrong name is not an invented quote and the byte check cannot catch it. Call 2 misses on the closed roster list honestly. **The resolver service (the twenty-second stamp's item 5, the resolution half of call 0, callable) is invoked with the designator** and runs the deterministic ladder. The resolved Participant/Contact ID attaches to the pair. Call 3 verifies the proposition blind, unchanged. The join writes the claim keyed to the resolved person, so the ID is in hand before any claim is attributed, which is Matthew's stated ordering. *(Twenty-seventh stamp: this flow's mechanics move INTO the rebuilt resolution stage unchanged; "call 2 misses honestly" becomes "the deterministic legs attach or the model leg requests.")*

**3 · THE NEW LADDER RUNG · TITLE LOOKUP, deterministic, scoped, Matthew's own sketch ratified.** Contact where Title matches the designator, scoped to the account, plus the contacts already on map rows for the account or opportunity (his `contactMapId.contact.title` clause). Two build requirements on the rung: **title normalization is a deterministic synonym list, never a model** ("CFO" and "Chief Financial Officer" are one title; ship the list as seed metadata, org-overridable, like the ontology); and the outcomes are the ladder's standard three: **exactly one match LINKS; more than one is AMBIGUOUS, the Identification flag, pair held, never pick; zero is "we did our best," Matthew's phrase, pair HELD with its evidence and reason, never dropped and never guessed.** The information is never lost, which was the point of his original ruling.

**4 · "A FEW REASONABLE TRIES" MADE PRECISE, because it is the difference between a ladder and a loop:** the tries are the ladder's rungs, run once, in order, each journalled. Never a model retrying until something matches. The rung order for a designator: exact name against the closed candidate set (map rows, Contacts, shadows, roster), the deterministic name parse, the title rung scoped as above, the alias. Exhausted = held with the flag.

**5 · THE FREE STRUCTURAL GUARD, and it kills the scariest failure of this whole path: A TITLE CAN LINK BUT CAN NEVER CREATE.** A Contact requires a LastName and "CFO" does not have one. So the two fuzziest designators, titles and pronouns, are structurally incapable of reaching the creation leg: a pronoun resolves to an in-artifact antecedent first and inherits that antecedent's kind, and a title dead-ends at link-or-held. **Only a real name reaches creation, and that path already sits behind ladder-zero, the internal check, and the create-leg record.** The ASR-name hazard binds only where it always did.

**6 · THE INTERNAL CHECK REACHES RESOLVED DESIGNATORS.** Whoever a pronoun or title resolves TO passes the name-based internal check like anyone else. "He" resolving to someone on our side refuses exactly the way Pat must. buq2 stays the acceptance case.
